> ॥ अष्टेक—४ ॥ अध्याय— १ ॥ वर्ग—४ ॥
> सूक्तदल्लिरुव ऋक्संख्यॆ—६
> ॥ ऋषिः—सुतंभर आत्रेयः ॥
> ॥ देवता—अग्निः ॥
> ॥ छंदः—त्रिष्टुप् ॥

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्राग्नये॑ बृह॒ते य॒ज्ञिया॑य ऋ॒तस्य॒ वृष्णे॒ असु॑राय॒ मन्म॑ ।  
> घृ॒तं न य॒ज्ञ आ॒स्ये॒३॒॑ ३ सुपू॑तं॒ गिरं॑ भरे वृष॒भाय॑  ॒
> प्रतीचीं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । अग्नये । बृहते । यज्ञियाय । ऋतस्य । वृष्णे ! असुराय । मन्म  
> धृतं । न । यजॆ ी । आस्ये । सुपूतं । गिरं । भरे । वृषभाय । प्रतीचीं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> बृहते सामर्थ्यातिशयेन महते यज्ञियाय यज्ञार्हाय ऋतस्कोदकस्य वृष्णे वर्षित्रे । ऋतमित्युदकनामसु पाठात् । असुराय बलवते ॥ असेर्णादिक उरन्प्रत्ययः ॥ वृषभाय कामानां वर्षित्रेऽग्नये मन्म ज्ञानसाधनं
> प्रतीचीमभिमुखीं गिरं स्तुतिरूपां वाचं प्रभरे । प्रणयामि । कथमिव ।
> सुपूतं सम्यक्संस्कृतमास्यॆ प्रहुतं यज्ञे धृतं न धृतमिव । आस्ये प्रहुतं धृतं
> यथा प्रीतिकरं भवति तथा प्रीतिकरीं स्तुतिमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

To great Agni, great through excess of ability, worthy of sacrifice, the showerer of ṛta (ṛta is water, as it is read among the names of water), the strong (asura means mighty), the showerer of desires, I bring forward an utterance in the form of praise, a means of knowledge, directed to him. How? As ghee, well purified, poured into the mouth at the sacrifice is pleasing to him, so I bring a praise that is pleasing.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यज्ञे (*yajñe*)—at the time of the sacrifice
- सुपूतं (*supūtaṃ*)—well purified
- धृतं (*dhṛtaṃ*)—the stream of ghee **NOTE**
- आस्येन (*āsyena*)—as one offers it into the mouth of Agni
- बृहते (*bṛhate*)—to him who has the nature of greatness
- यज्ञियाय (*yajñiyāya*)—worthy of sacrifice
### Printed p. 43
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of water
- वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)—the causer of rain (the one who pours down rain)
- असुराय (*asurāya*)—endowed with strength
- वृषभाय (*vṛṣabhāya*)—the showerer of desires
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—to Agni
- मन्म (*manma*)—that which brings about reflection
- प्रतीचीं (*pratīcīṃ*)—that which comes to meet him
- गिरं (*giraṃ*)—the praise
- प्रभरे (*prabhare*)—I offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As, at the time of the sacrifice, a stream of well-purified ghee is offered into the mouth of Agni, so I offer, to Agni, who has the nature of greatness, is worthy of sacrifice, causes rain, is endowed with strength and showers desires, a praise that brings about reflection and comes to meet him.

##### English translation
I offer to the great, the adorable Agni. the rainer of water, the vigorous, the showerer (of benefits), the present acceptable praise, like well-purified butter (poured) at sacrifices into his mouth.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

बृहते—सामर्थ्यातिशयेन महते । (*bṛhate—sāmarthyātiśayena mahate |*) The abilities of Agni are many: the power to give the desired objects to those who worship him, the power to destroy enemies, the power to carry the oblations and other materials that the sacrificers offer in him to the gods for whom they are intended, and many other kinds of power and ability. Because Agni has these, he is possessed of excess of ability, and so the great nature of Agni is set out here.
असुराय (*asurāya*)—The word asura has many meanings. Here असुराय—बलवते । (*asurāya—balavate |*) the word is used in the sense of strength.
प्रतीचीं (*pratīcīṃ*)—This word is an adjective of the word गिरं (*giraṃ*). The main purport is: I stand facing Agni and recite the words of praise.
सुपूतं धृतं आस्स्येन (*supūtaṃ dhṛtaṃ āssyena*)—Here an illustration is given for reciting the hymn while standing facing Agni. It is a matter of experience that when ghee (ghṛta) is offered in Agni it burns blazing and shining; that is, the idea is suggested that Agni is pleased by a stream of ghee. Therefore, in the way in which Agni is pleased when ghee is offered in the mouth of Agni, that is, in the Agni blazing with flames, in that way the seer describes: may Agni be pleased with the words of praise that I, the seer, recite standing facing Agni.
### Printed p. 44
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऋ॒तं चि॑कित्स ऋ॒तमिच्चि॑किद्द्य॒तस्य॒ धारा॒ अनु॑ तृंधि पू॒र्वीः ।  
> नाहं या॒तुं सह॑सा॒ न द्व॒येन॑ ऋ॒तं स॑साम्यरु॒षस्य॒ वृष्णः॑॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ऋतं । चिकित्वः । ऋतं । इत् । चिकिद्दि । ऋतस्य । धाराः । अनु । तृंधि । पूर्वीः ।  
> न । अहं । यातुं । सहसा । न । द्वयेन । ऋतं । सपामि । अरुषस्य । वृष्टः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋतं स्तोत्रं हे चिकित्सो जानन्नग्ने त्वं ऋतं मया क्रियमाणमिदं
> स्तोत्रं चिकिद्धि । जानीहि ॥ कित ज्ञाने । जुहोत्यादिः । लोटि रूपं । इदिति पूरणः ।
> ऋतमिति पदस्य परस्मिन्नामंत्रिते विद्यमानेऽपि छांदसत्वात्परांगवद्भावाभावः॥ किंच
> पूर्वर्बह्वीऋतस्कोदकस्य धारास्तस्य पोषणार्थमुत्पादयितमनु तृंधि ।
> मेघाननुविध्य । सहसा बलेन युक्तोऽहं यातुं कर्मणां नाशकरीं हिंसां न
> सपामि । न स्पृशामि । न करोमीति यावत् । द्वयेन च सत्यानृताभ्यां यत्किंचिदवैदिकं कृत्यं न सपामि ॥ षप समवाय इति धातुः ॥ किंतु अरुषस्य रोचमानस्य
> वृष्णः कामानां वर्षितुस्तव ऋतं स्तोत्रमेव सपामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

ṛta here means praise. O Agni, who know (cikitvaḥ), know this praise made by me. Further, to nourish the world, to bring about the abundant streams of ṛta, that is, of water, pierce (tṛndhi) the clouds. I, endowed with strength, do not touch (sapāmi), that is, I do not practise, the violence that destroys sacrificial rites; and with the two, truth and untruth, I do not do any act that is not Vedic. But I address only praise to you, the radiant one, the showerer of desires.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—my praise
- चिकित्सः (*cikitsaḥ*)—O Agni, who know
- ऋतुं (*ṛtuṃ*)—this praise composed by me
- चिकिद्दि (*cikiddi*)—praise fully
- पूर्विः (*pūrviḥ*)—abundantly
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of water
- धाराः (*dhārāḥ*)—the streams
- अनु त्वंधि (*anu tvaṃdhi*)—cause (from the cloud) to pour down
- सहसा (*sahasā*)—endowed with strength
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- यातुं (*yātuṃ*)—the act that obstructs the rite
- न सपामि (*na sapāmi*)—I do not practise
- द्वयेन (*dvayena*)—either by truth or by untruth
- न (*na*)—I do not trouble (but)
- अरुषस्य (*aruṣasya*)—of you who have a radiant colour
- वृष्णः (*vṛṣṇaḥ*)—the showerer of desires
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—praise itself (I practise)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you who know the excellence of my praise, praise this hymn composed by me. Cause streams of water to pour abundantly from the cloud. I, from pride in my strength,
### Printed p. 45
do not practise obstruction of the rite. Either by truth or by untruth, I do not trouble the rite. But I compose and recite praise of you, who have a radiant colour and shower desires.

##### English translation
Agni, who know (the purpose of) the sacrifice, assent to this rite: be favourable to the copious showers of water : (endowed) with strength, (I undertake) not (to obstruct) sacred acts, nor adjure the rite with both (truth and untruth), but (repeat the praise) of the resplendent showerer (of benefits).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The meaning of this Ṛk is not so clear. To make the meaning clear, many words have been supplied, and an effort has been made so that a definite sense becomes evident. Only some of them are explained here.
ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—In this Ṛk this word is read three times. For this word ṛta Sāyaṇa gives the meaning "this hymn made by me (the seer)". For the word ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*) he gives the meaning उदकस्य (*udakasya*), "of the waters". For the use of the word dhārāḥ, "streams of water", in ऋतस्य धाराः (*ṛtasya dhārāḥ*) indicates that the word ṛtasya means water (or ghee?).
यातुं—कर्मणां नाशकरीं हिंसां । (*yātuṃ—karmaṇāṃ nāśakarīṃ hiṃsāṃ |*) He has fitted the meaning: the violence that causes obstruction to the sacrificial rite.
द्वयेन (*dvayena*)—Here also an explanation is given that the two are two kinds of rite, truth and untruth.
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> कया॑ नो अग्न ऋ॒तय॑न्नृ॒तेन॒ भुवो॒ नवे॑दा उ॒चथ॑स्य॒  ॑
> नव्यः ।  
> वेदा॑ मे दे॒व ऋ॑तु॒पा ऋ॑तू॒नां नाहं पतिं॑ सनि॒तुर॒स्य  ॒
> रायः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 46

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कया । नः । अग्ने । ऋतयन् । ऋतेन । भुवः । नवेदाः । उचथस्य । नव्यः ।  
> वेद । मे । ' देवः । ऋतुपाः । ऋतूनां । न । अहं । पतिं । सनितुः । अस्य ।  
> रायः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने ऋतयन् सर्वप्राणिनां जीवनहेतुकमुदकं कुर्वन् कया
> केनाप्यृतेन सत्येन क्रियमाणीन कर्मणा न उचथस्यास्मदीयस्य स्तोत्रस्य नवेदा
> ज्ञाता भुवः । भवेः । नव्यः स्तुत्य ऋतूनां ऋतूपाः । द्वितीय ऋतुशब्दोऽनुवादकः । देवो द्योतमानोऽग्निर्मे मां वेद वेत्तु । सनितुर्भजमानस्यास्य
> मम रायः पश्वादिलक्षणस्य पतिं स्वामिनं तमग्निमहं न जानामि ॥ भुवः । भू
> सत्तायां । लिजर्थे लेट्यडागमः । उवजादेशः । नवेदाः । न न वेत्तीति वेत्तीत्यस्मिन्नर्थे वर्तते । कथमेतल्लभ्यते । निपातनात् । द्विनञ्पूर्वस्यासुन्प्रत्ययांतस्य
> वेदेरेकस्य नइतो लोपोऽन्यस्य प्रकृतिभावश्च स्कंदस्वामिना निपातितः । पाणिनिना
> तु नभ्राण्णपादिति सूत्रेन वेत्तीत्येतस्मिन्नर्थे निपातितः । अस्यार्थस्यानुपपन्न त्वात्
> स्कंदस्वामिपक्ष एवाश्रीयते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, who make water, the means of life of all beings, by what rite performed with truth do you become the knower of our praise? The praiseworthy one, the guardian of the seasons (the second word for "season" is only a repetition), the shining Agni, may he know me. Of me, the one who serves him, whose wealth consists of cattle and the like, I do not know that Agni, the lord. He explains नवेदाः (*navedāḥ*) as having the sense "he knows" (not "he does not know"), which is obtained by an irregular formation; he adopts the view of Skandasvāmin, while Pāṇini by the sūtra नभ्राण्णपादिति (*nabhrāṇṇapāditi*) gives the word a sense that does not fit here, so that the sense is not suitable. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ऋतयन् (*ṛtayan*)—providing water (to all beings)
- कया ऋतेन (*kayā ṛtena*)—by what rite, of the nature of truth
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- उचथस्य (*ucathasya*)—of the praise
- नवेदा: (*navedā:*)—as the knower
- भुवः (*bhuvaḥ*)—do you become?
- नव्यः (*navyaḥ*)—the praiseworthy one
- ऋतूनां (*ṛtūnāṃ*)—of all the seasons
- ऋतुपाः (*ṛtupāḥ*)—the guardian
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the shining one, Agni
- मे (*me*)—me
- वेद (*veda*)—may he know (as his devotee)
- सनितु: (*sanitu:*)—endowed with wealth
- अस्य (*asya*)—my
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—of wealth
- पतिं (*patiṃ*)—the lord, Agni alone
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- न (*na*)—do not yet know

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you who provide water to all beings and do good to the world, which of our rites can make you know our praise? May Agni, the praiseworthy one, the guardian of the seasons, the shining one, know me as his devotee. Only Agni, the lord of my wealth, I have not yet seen face to face.
### Printed p. 47

##### English translation
Agni, bestower of water, by what (act of) truth may you, who are adorable, be cognizant of our adoration ? may the divine Agni, the guardian of the seasons recognize me : I (know) him not (yet) as the lord of the riches of which I am the possessor.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk also the word ṛtu is used four times. For the first, ऋतुयन् (*ṛtuyan*), the meaning given is water; for the second, ऋतेन (*ṛtena*), a rite of the nature of truth; for the third and the fourth, the words ऋतूनां ऋतुपाः (*ṛtūnāṃ ṛtupāḥ*), the meaning given is the seasons (words denoting time). Here there is a repetition, ऋतुपा ऋतूनां (*ṛtupā ṛtūnāṃ*). Such repeated expressions are specially found in the Ṛgveda: रयिपतिं रयाणां (*rayipatiṃ rayāṇāṃ*); विशां विश्वतिं (*viśāṃ viśvatiṃ*); रयाणां त्वा रयिपतिं (*rayāṇāṃ tvā rayipatiṃ*); वसूनां वसुपतिं (*vasūnāṃ vasupatiṃ*), and so on.
सनितुः अस्य रायः पतिं अहं न—भजमानस्य मम पश्वादिलक्षणस्य धनस्य स्वामिनं तमग्निं नाहं जानामि । (*sanituḥ asya rāyaḥ patiṃ ahaṃ na—bhajamānasya mama paśvādilakṣaṇasya dhanasya svāminaṃ tamagniṃ nāhaṃ jānāmi |*) Agni is the lord of wealth. This wealth is of many kinds, such as cattle, food and houses. The seer says that he (being of little understanding) does not know well the qualities and surpassing powers of Agni, the lord of such wealth, and thus expresses the greatness of Agni and his own smallness.
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> के ते॑ अग्ने रि॒पवे॒ बंध॑नासः॒ के पा॒यवः॑ सनिषंत द्यु॒मंतः॑।  
> के धा॒सिम॑ग्ने॒ अनृ॑तस्य पांति॒ क आस॑तो॒ वच॑सः संति गो॒पाः॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> के । ते । अग्ने । रिपवे । बंधनासः । के । पायवः । सनिषंत । द्युमंतः ।  
> के । धासिं । अग्ने । अनृतस्य । पांति । के । असतः । वचसः । संति । गोपाः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

### Printed p. 48

> हे अग्ने ये रिपवे स्वकीयस्य शत्रोः बंधनासो बंधकाः स्युस्ते तत्संबंधिनस्ते पुरुषाः के कीदृशाः । सामर्थातिशयादेवंविधा इति केनापि ज्ञातुं न
> शक्यंत इत्यर्थः । ये पायवो जनानां रक्षितारो द्युमंतो दीप्तिमंतः सनिषंत
> दीनेभ्यो धनं प्रयच्छंति त्वदीयाः के कीदृशाः । येऽनृतस्यासत्यस्य धासिं धारकं
> जनं पांति रक्षंति ते त्वदीया जनाः के संति । न केऽपीत्यर्थः । येऽसतो दुष्टस्याभिशापादिलक्षणस्य वचसो गोपा गॊपायितारो रक्षितारस्ते त्वदीयाः के संति ।
> न केऽपीत्यर्थः । अथवा ये के जना रिपोर्बंधकाः । ये के पायवो रक्षितार्
> सनिषंत धनं प्रयच्छंति द्युमंतः । ये केऽनृतस्यासत्यस्य धासिं धारकं जनं पांति
> अनृतजनितपापात्परिहृत्य सन्मार्गप्रदानेन रक्षंति । ये केऽसतोऽभिशापादिलक्षणाद्वचसो गोपा गॊपायितारः संति ते सर्वॆ त्वदीयाः । त्वदुपासका इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, who are those of your people that would be the binders of one's enemy? By reason of their surpassing power no one can know of what kind such men are: this is the meaning. Who among yours are the radiant protectors of people, who give wealth to the poor? Who of yours protect the man who bears untruth? Who are there as guardians of evil speech, such as a curse? None at all: this is the meaning. Or else: whoever are the binders of the enemy; whoever are the protectors who give wealth and are radiant; whoever protect the man who bears untruth by turning him away from the sin born of untruth and giving him the right path; whoever are guardians of evil speech of the nature of curses and the like: all of these are yours, that is, your worshippers.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ते (*te*)—among the men who belong to you
- रिपवे (*ripave*)—to the enemy
- बंधनासः (*baṃdhanāsaḥ*)—the binders
- के (*ke*)—who?
- द्युमंतः (*dyumaṃtaḥ*)—the radiant
- पायवः (*pāyavaḥ*)—the protectors of the poor
- के (*ke*)—who
- सनिषंत (*saniṣaṃta*)—give wealth to the afflicted?
- अनृतस्य (*anṛtasya*)—of untruth
- धासिं (*dhāsiṃ*)—the bearer, the man
- के (*ke*)—who
- पांति (*pāṃti*)—protect?
- असतः (*asataḥ*)—of wicked
- वचसः (*vacasaḥ*)—speech
- गोपाः (*gopāḥ*)—the guardians
- के संति (*ke saṃti*)—who are there?

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, among the company of men who belong to you, who are the good and who are the bad? How many are there who bind enemies and give wealth to the afflicted, who are radiant and protectors of the poor? How many are there who protect the man of untruthful nature and his evil deeds? Who are they?

##### English translation
Who, Agni, among your (followers) are the imprisoners of foes ? who among them are the protectors (of men), the splendid distributors of gifts ? who among them defend the asserter of untruth ? who are the encouragers of evil deeds ?

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

O Agni, those who conquer and bind the enemy, the protectors of the poor, those who give wealth to the afflicted, those who protect, without seeing, those who have spoken untruth, **NOTE** and those who have gained good sense so as not to speak evil words: all such good men are yours, that is, they are those who worship you. This is the main purport.
### Printed p. 49
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सखा॑यस्ते॒ विषु॑णा अग्न ए॒ते शि॒वासः॒ संतो॒ आशि॑वा अभूवन्।  
> अधू॑र्षत स्व॒यमे॒ते वचो॑भिर्ऋजूय॒ते वृ॑जि॒नानि॑  ॒॑
> ब्रुवंतः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सखायः । ते । विषुणाः । अग्ने । एते । शिवासः । संतः । अशिवाः । अभूवन् ।  
> अधूर्षत । स्वयं । एते । वचःभिः । ऋजुयते । वृजिनानि । ब्रुवंतः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने ते तव सखायः स्तोतारो विषुणा विप्रकीर्णाः सर्वत्र व्याप्ता
> एते पूर्वमशिवाः परनिंदादिकुर्वाणास्त्वत्परिचर्यां त्यजंतोऽभद्राः संत
> इदानीं त्वत्परिचर्यां कुर्वंतः शिवासः शिवा आध्या अभूवन् । किंच य ऋजूयत
> ऋजु सम्यगाचरतेऽपि मह्यं वचोभिरसाधूक्तिभिर्वृजिनानि कुटिलानि ब्रुवंतस्त
> एते मम सपत्नाः स्वयमेवाधूर्षत । अहिंस्यंत ॥ धूर्वतेर्हिंसाकर्मण इदं
> रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, these friends of yours, your praisers, dispersed and spread everywhere, who were formerly inauspicious, reviling others and the like and abandoning your service, and so unfortunate, have now become auspicious and fortunate, rendering you service. Moreover, those who, to me who conduct myself straight and rightly, speak crooked things with words, that is, with evil speech: these my rivals may themselves be harmed. The word अहिंस्यंत (*ahiṃsyaṃta*) is given as the sense of the verb form.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- विषुणाः (*viṣuṇāḥ*)—dispersed and settled everywhere
- एते ते सखायः (*ete te sakhāyaḥ*)—these men, friends of yours
- अशिवाः संतः (*aśivāḥ saṃtaḥ*)—(formerly, not worshipping you) being doers of inauspicious acts; (now)
- शिवासः (*śivāsaḥ*)—in the auspicious work of your worship
- अभूवन् (*abhūvan*)—they are engaged
- ऋजूयते (*ṛjūyate*)—to me, the doer of good acts
- वचोभिः (*vacobhiḥ*)—with evil words
- वृजिनानि (*vṛjināni*)—deceitful acts
- ब्रुवंतः (*bruvaṃtaḥ*)—who practise
- एते (*ete*)—these my enemies
- स्वयं (*svayaṃ*)—of their own accord
- अधूर्षत (*adhūrṣata*)—let them bring harm upon themselves

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, these men, friends of yours, dispersed and settled everywhere, who formerly, not worshipping you, were doers of inauspicious acts, are now engaged in the auspicious work of your worship.
### Printed p. 50
Let these my enemies, who practise deceitful acts upon me, the doer of good acts, by the use of evil words, bring harm upon themselves of their own accord.

##### English translation
These your friends, Agni, everywhere dispersed, were formerly unhappy (in abandoning your worship), but are again fortunate (by its renewal); may they who, with (censorous) words, impute fraudulent (practices) to me who pursue a straight path, bring evil upon themselves.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Those who are now good men were formerly, that is, before worshipping Agni, wicked and used to do evil deeds. All such wicked people, having worshipped Agni and thereby gained good sense, have become good men. But for all those who have not become good in that way by serving Agni, the wicked people who are my enemies and deceive me, may harm come according to the fruit of their deeds. This is the main purport.
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्ते॑ अग्ने॒ नम॑सा य॒ज्ञमाट्ट॑ ऋ॒तं स पा॑त्यरु॒षस्य॒ वृष्णः॑ ।  
> तस्य॒ क्षयः॑ पृ॒थुरा सा॒धुरे॑तु प्र॒सर्सा॑णस्य॒ नहु॑षस्य॒ शेषः॑ ॥६॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । ते । अग्ने । नमसा । यज्ञं । ईट्टे । ऋतं । सः । पाति । अरुषस्य । वृष्णः ।  
> तस्य । क्षयः । पृथुः । आ । साधुः । एतु । प्रसस्राणस्य । नहुषस्य । शेषः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने यो मनुष्यो यज्ञं यजनीयं ते त्वां नमसा स्तोत्रॆणेट्टे
> स्तौति । यद्वा । ते तुभ्यं यज्ञं यागं नमसान्नरूपेण हविषेट्टे यजति ॥
> अस्मिन् पक्षे यजेर्बहुलं छंदसीति शपः श्लुः । श्लाविति द्विर्वचनं । बहुलं
> छंदसीति संप्रसारणं । सवर्णदीर्घः । जकारस्य टकारः ॥ योऽरुषस्य रोचमानस्य

### Printed p. 51

> वृष्णः कामानां वर्षितुरग्नेर्युतं स्तोत्रं पाति रक्षति तस्य यजमानस्य क्षयो
> गृहं पृथुर्विस्तीर्णं भवति । पश्वादिलक्षणैर्धनैः संपूर्णं भवतीत्यर्थः । प्रसर्स्राणस्य प्रकर्षेण परिचर्या० गच्छतो नहुषस्य मनुष्यस्य साधुः कामानां
> साधकः शेषः । शिष्यत इति शेषः । पुत्र ऐतु । आगच्छतु । भवत्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, the man who praises you, the one worthy of worship, with reverent praise; or else, the man who worships you with the oblation in the form of food, offering the sacrifice to you. (The explanation of the verbal form for this second view is a matter of grammar.) He protects the praise belonging to Agni, the shining one, the showerer of desires. The house of that sacrificer becomes extensive, that is, it becomes full of wealth such as cattle. To the man who goes forth in service of you, may a virtuous remnant, one who accomplishes desires, come: what remains is called the remnant, that is, a son; the meaning is "may he come, may he be".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the man who
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—worthy of worship
- ते (*te*)—you
- नमसा (*namasā*)—with praise
- ईट्वे (*īṭve*)—praises
- सः (*saḥ*)—he
- अरुषस्य (*aruṣasya*)—of the one who has a radiant lustre **NOTE**
- वृष्णः (*vṛṣṇaḥ*)—of Agni, the showerer of desires
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—the praise that is a part of the sacrifice
- पाति (*pāti*)—protects
- तस्य (*tasya*)—of that same sacrificer
- क्षयः (*kṣayaḥ*)—the dwelling-house
- पृथुः (*pṛthuḥ*)—grows extensive
- प्रसर्साणस्य (*prasarsāṇasya*)—who worships you most excellently
- नहुषस्य (*nahuṣasya*)—to the man
- साधु: (*sādhu:*)—of good understanding
- शेषः (*śeṣaḥ*)—a son
- आ एतु (*ā etu*)—may he be born

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the man who praises you, the one worthy of worship, with reverent praise protects the praise, a part of the sacrifice, of Agni, who is radiant and a showerer of desires. The dwelling-house of that same sacrificer grows extensive. To the man who worships you most excellently, may a son of good understanding be born.

##### English translation
May the dwelling of him, Agni, who celebrates your worship with reverence, of him who protects the sacrifice (offered) to the resplendent showerer (of benefits), be well stored and may a virtuous successor of the man who diligently worships you come in his place.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the two preceding Ṛks the seer told of the troubles that come to those who do not worship Agni; in this Ṛk he describes the fruit that comes to those who praise Agni and offer oblations. The houses of the good men who, offering oblations to Agni, the one worthy of sacrifice, worship with salutation and praise the shining Agni, the giver of what is desired, will prosper. Prosperity in cattle and the like will come to them. A good and worthy son will be born to them: so the seer has described.

## Sūkta 13 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अर्चंतस्त्वोति षडृचं त्रयोदशं सूक्तं सुतंभरस्यार्षं गायत्रमाग्नेयं ।
> अनुक्रांतं च । अर्चंतो गायत्रं त्विति ॥ प्रातरनुवाक आग्नेयक्रत् गायत्रछंदस्याश्विनशस्त्र इदमादिके द्वे सूक्ते । सूत्रितं च । अर्चंतस्त्वोति सूक्ते ।
> आ. ४-१३ । इति ॥ अहवनीयोपस्थाने त्वमग्ने सप्रथा आसीत्येका । सूत्रितं च । सं
> नः सृज सुमत्या वाजवत्या त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असीति च । आ. ३-१० । इति ॥ अश्वमेधे
> पौष्टेष्टौ त्वमग्ने सप्रथा इति प्रथमाज्यभागानुवाक्या । त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असि सोम
> यास्ते मयोभुव इति सद्वंतौ । आ. १०-६ । इति ॥
> अनुवादवु—अर्चंतस्त्वा ऎंब ई सूक्तवु मॊदलनॆय अनुवाकदल्लि हदिमूरनॆय
> सूक्तवु. इदरल्लि आरु ऋक्कुगळिरुववु. ई सूक्तक्कॆ सुतंभर आत्रेयः ऎंबुवनु ऋषियु ;
> अग्नियु देवतॆयु ; गायत्रीछंदस्सु. अनुक्रमणिकॆयल्लि—अर्चंतो गायत्रं तु ऎंदु
> हेळिरुवुदु. प्रातरनुवाकमंत्रपठनदल्लि आग्नेयक्रतुसंबंधवाद गायत्रीछंदस्सिन मंत्र
> गळिगागियू, आश्विनशस्त्रमंत्रपठनक्कागियू ई सूक्त मत्तु मुंदिन सूक्तद विनियोगविरुवु
> दॆंदु आश्वलायनशौतसूत्रद—अर्चंतस्त्वति सूक्ते ऎंब सूत्रदिंद (आ. ४-१३) विवृत
> वागिरुवुदु ; आहवनीयाग्नि यन्नु उपस्थानमाडुवाग-त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असि ऎंब ( ई सूक्तद
> ४ नॆय) ऋक्कन्नु पठिसबेकॆंदु आश्वलायशौतसूत्रद—सं नः सृज सुमत्या वाजवत्या
> त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असीति च ऎंब सूत्रदिंद (आ. ३-१०) विवृतवागिरुवुदु. मत्तु अश्वमेध
> यागद पौष्टॆष्टियल्लि (पूषन् ऎंब देवतॆयन्नुद्देशिसि माडुव इष्टियल्लि) त्वमग्ने सप्रथा
> ऎंब ई ऋक्कन्नु प्रथमाज्यभागद अनुवाक्यामंत्रवन्नागि पठिसबेकॆंदु आश्वलायनशौत
> सूत्रद—त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असि सोम यास्ते मयो भुव इति सद्वंतौ ऎंब सूत्रदिंद
> (आ. १०-६) विवृतवागिरुवुदु.

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The thirteenth sūkta, अर्चंतस्त्वोति (*arcaṃtastvoti*), has six Ṛks; its seer is Sutaṃbhara, its metre Gāyatrī, its deity Agni. The Anukramaṇī says: अर्चंतो गायत्रं त्विति (*arcaṃto gāyatraṃ tviti*). In the Prātaranuvāka, in the Āgneya rite, for the verses in Gāyatrī metre, and in the Āśvina-śastra, the two sūktas beginning with this one are used. It is prescribed in the sūtra: अर्चंतस्त्वोति सूक्ते (*arcaṃtastvoti sūkte*) (Ā. Sū. 4-13). At the worship of the Āhavanīya fire, one Ṛk, त्वमग्ने सप्रथा (*tvamagne saprathā*), is used; as the sūtra says: सं नः सृज सुमत्या वाजवत्या त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असीति च (*saṃ naḥ sṛja sumatyā vājavatyā tvamagne saprathā asīti ca*) (Ā. Sū. 3-10). In the Pauṣṭa offering (iṣṭi) of the Aśvamedha, त्वमग्ने सप्रथा (*tvamagne saprathā*) is the first Ājyabhāga anuvākyā, and सोम यास्ते मयोभुव इति सद्वंतौ (*soma yāste mayobhuva iti sadvaṃtau*) (Ā. Sū. 10-6).

> ऋषि:--सुतंभर आत्रेयः
> देवता—आग्निः ॥
> ॥ छंदः—गायत्रि ॥

**NOTE**
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अर्चं॑तस्त्वा हवाम॒हेऽर्चं॑तः॒ समि॑धीमहि ।  
> अग्ने॒ अर्चं॑त ऊ॒तये॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अर्चंतः । त्वा । हवामहे । अर्चंतः । सं । इधीमहि ।  
> अग्ने । अर्चंतः । ऊतये ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वा त्वामर्चंतः पूजयंतो वयं हवामहे । आह्वयामः ।
> अर्चंतः स्तुवंतो वयमूतये रक्षणाय तर्पणाय वा त्वां समिधीमहि ।
> समिद्धिः संदीपयामः । अर्चंत इति तृतीयं पदमादरातिशयार्थं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, we who adore you invoke you. Adoring, that is, praising, we kindle you with fuel-sticks, we make you blaze, for our protection or for your satisfaction. The word अर्चंत (*arcaṃta*) stands a third time for the sake of great respect.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- अर्चंतः (*arcaṃtaḥ*)—we who worship
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we call you for help
- अर्चंतः (*arcaṃtaḥ*)—praising you
- उतये (*utaye*)—for protection
- समिधीमहि (*samidhīmahi*)—we make you blaze

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, worshipping you, we pray and call you for our help. Praising you, we make you blaze for our protection.
### Printed p. 54

##### English translation
Adoring you, Agni, we invoke you: adoring you we kindle you, adoring you for protection.
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्नेः स्तोमं॑ मनामहे सि॒ध्रम॒द्य दि॑वि॒स्पृशः॑ ।  
> दे॒वस्य॑ द्रविण॒स्यवः॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्नेः । स्तोमं । मनामहे । सिध्रं । अद्य । दिविस्पृशः ।  
> देवस्य । द्रविणस्यवः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्रविणस्यवो द्रविणं धनमिच्छंतो वयं दिविस्पृशः सूर्यरूपेणाकाशं
> व्याप्नुवतो देवस्य द्योतमानस्याग्नेः सिध्रं पुरुषार्थानां साधकं स्तोमं
> स्तोत्रमद्यास्मिन्नहनि मनामहे । ब्रूमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We who desire wealth recite today, on this day, the praise of the shining Agni, who pervades the sky in the form of the sun: a praise that accomplishes the aims of man.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- द्रविणस्यवः (*draviṇasyavaḥ*)—we who desire wealth
- दिविस्पृशः (*divispṛśaḥ*)—of him who pervades the intermediate space in the form of the sun
- देवस्य (*devasya*)—and who is shining
- अग्नेः (*agneḥ*)—the praise that is addressed to Agni
- सिद्धं (*siddhaṃ*)—that accomplishes the aims of man
- स्तोमं (*stomaṃ*)—praise
- अद्य (*adya*)—at this time of the sacrifice
- मनामहे (*manāmahe*)—we recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We who desire wealth recite, at the time of this sacrifice, the praise addressed to the shining Agni, who pervades the intermediate space in the form of the sun, the praise that accomplishes the aims of man.

##### English translation
Desirous of wealth, we recite to-day the effectual praise of the divine Agni, (whose radiance) reaches the sky.
### Printed p. 55

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

It has been explained many times before that Agni pervades in three forms: in heaven in the form of Āditya, in the intermediate space in the form of lightning-fire, and on earth in the form of terrestrial fire (the sacrificial fire, the household fire or the forest fire). In the present Ṛk it is explained that Agni shines in heaven (the intermediate space) in the form of the sun.
सिद्ध्रं-साधनं । (*siddhraṃ-sādhanaṃ |*) Yāska has explained that the word sidhra has the meaning "means" (Ni. 9-38), and as an example has cited the mantra **NOTE** द्यावा नः पृथिवी इमं सिध्रमद्य दिविस्स,शं । यज्ञं देवेषु यच्छतां ॥ (*dyāvā naḥ pṛthivī imaṃ sidhramadya divissa,śaṃ | yajñaṃ deveṣu yacchatāṃ ‖*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 2-41-20).
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निर्जु॑षत नोः॒ गिरो॒ होता॒ यो मानु॑षे॒ष्वा ।  
> स य॑क्ष॒द्दैव्यं॒ जनं॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । जुषत । नः । गिरः । होता । यः । मानुषेषु । आ ।  
> सः । यक्षत् । दैव्यं । जनं ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> होता देवानामाह्वाता योऽग्निर्मानुषेष्वा वसति सोऽग्निर्नोऽस्माकं
> गिरः स्तुतीर्जाषत । सेवतां । सोऽग्निर्दैव्यं जनं' देवसंबंधिनं जनं यक्षत् ।
> यजतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Hotā, the invoker of the gods, who dwells among men: may that Agni accept our praises, that is, partake of them. May that Agni offer sacrifice to the people connected with the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the one who
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- मानुषेषु (*mānuṣeṣu*)—stands among men
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—words in the form of praise
- जुषत (*juṣata*)—may he partake of (and)
- दैव्यं जनं (*daivyaṃ janaṃ*)—with reference to people of divine nature
- यक्षत् (*yakṣat*)—may he perform the sacrifice
### Printed p. 56

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May that Agni who stands in the midst of men partake of our praises, and may he perform the sacrifice with reference to the people of divine nature.

##### English translation
May Agni, who amongst men is the invoker of the gods, accept our praises ; may he offer sacrifice to the divine beings-

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

यः अग्निः मानुषेषु आ (*yaḥ agniḥ mānuṣeṣu ā*)—the Agni who is among men, that is, the sacrificial fire established on the sacrificial altar in the form of Gārhapatya and the rest. In the preceding Ṛk the Agni who is in heaven in the form of Āditya was praised; in this Ṛk the terrestrial Agni is praised. Although the terrestrial fire has the varieties sacrificial fire, household fire and forest fire, here there is the prayer नः गिरः जुषत (*naḥ giraḥ juṣata*), "may he partake of our praises", and so it should be understood that the praise is addressed to the sacrificial fire alone.
दै व्यं जनं (*dai vyaṃ janaṃ*)—देवसंबंधिनं जनं (*devasaṃbaṃdhinaṃ janaṃ*): people connected with the gods, that is, the gods who are invoked in the sacrifice: such is the purport.
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वम॑ग्ने स॒प्रथा॑ असि॒ जुष्टो॒ होता॒ वरे॑ण्यः ।  
> त्वया॑ य॒ज्ञं वि त॑न्वते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । अग्ने । सप्रथाः । असि । जुष्टः । होता । वरेण्यः ।  
> श्वया । यज्ञं । वि । तन्नते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने जुष्टः सर्वदा प्रीतो वरेण्यः सर्वैर्वर्णनीयो होता त्वं
> सप्रथाः सर्वतः पृथुरसि । भवसि । तथाह यास्कः । सप्रथाः सर्वतः पृथुः । नि. ६. ७. ।
> इति । किंच सर्वे यजमानास्त्वाया साधनेन यज्ञं वितन्वते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you who are ever pleased, who are to be chosen by all, the Hotā, are spread widely everywhere. So Yāska says: सर्वतः पृथुः (*sarvataḥ pṛthuḥ*) (Ni. 6. 7.). Moreover, all sacrificers extend the sacrifice by you as the means.
### Printed p. 57

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- जुष्टः (*juṣṭaḥ*)—fully satisfied
- वरेण्यः (*vareṇyaḥ*)—honoured by all
- होता (*hotā*)—the accomplisher of the offering
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- सप्रथाः (*saprathāḥ*)—having gained pre-eminence in every way
- असि (*asi*)—you are
- त्वया (*tvayā*)—by your help alone
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—the sacrifice
- वि तन्वते (*vi tanvate*)—(the sacrificers) carry out

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you who are fully satisfied, honoured by all and the accomplisher of the offering, have gained pre-eminence in every way. By your help alone the sacrificers carry out the sacrifice.

##### English translation
You, Agni, are mighty and gracious, the (ministrant) priest, the desired (of all): through you worshippers complete the sacrifice.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

जुष्टः (*juṣṭaḥ*)—सर्वदा प्रीतः (*sarvadā prītaḥ*). The purport is: one who is always pleased, who is delighted, with the oblations and the like that the sacrificers offer in the sacrifice.
वरेण्यः (*vareṇyaḥ*)—सर्वैर्वर्णीयः (*sarvairvarṇīyaḥ*): one who is desired by all. Since a sacrifice cannot be performed without Agni, all people, the sacrificers and the rest, desire Agni; Agni is needed by all. Therefore in the second half of this Ṛk it is said त्वया यज्ञं वि तन्वते (*tvayā yajñaṃ vi tanvate*): all perform the sacrifice by you (with your help).
सप्रथाः (*saprathāḥ*)—सर्वतः पृथुः (*sarvataḥ pṛthuḥ*). त्वमग्ने सप्रथा असीत्यपि निगमो भवति (*tvamagne saprathā asītyapi nigamo bhavati*) (Ni. 6-7). Since Agni pervades everywhere (when he burns), he spreads very widely: thus Yāska explained the meaning of the word saprathāḥ and gave this very Ṛk as the example.
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वाम॑ग्ने वाज॒सात॑मं॒ विप्रा॑ वर्धंति॒ सुष्टु॑तं ।  
> स नो॑ रास्व सु॒वीर्यं॑ ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 58

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वां । अग्ने । वाजसातमं । विप्राः । वर्धंति । सुस्तुतं ।  
> सः । नः । रास्व । सुवीर्यं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने विप्रा मेधाविनः स्तोतारो वाजसातमं । वाजोऽन्नं । तद्ददतं
> सुष्टुतं पूर्वैर्महर्षिभिः सम्यक् स्तुतं तं त्वां वर्धंति । स्तोत्रैर्वर्धयंति ।
> स त्वं नोऽस्मभ्यं सुवीर्यं सर्वैः श्लाघनीयं बलं रास्व । देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, the wise praisers, the intelligent ones, make you grow with their hymns: you, the giver of food (vāja is food, and you give it), well praised by the great seers of old, thoroughly praised. May you, such as you are, give us excellent strength that is to be praised by all.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)—the wise praisers
- वाजसातमं (*vājasātamaṃ*)—the giver of food
- सुष्टुतं (*suṣṭutaṃ*)—praised by all
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- वर्धंति (*vardhaṃti*)—they cause to grow
- सः (*saḥ*)—such you
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सुवीर‍्यं (*suvīryaṃ*)—praiseworthy strength
- रास्व (*rāsva*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the wise praisers cause you to grow, you who are the giver of food and are praised by all. May you, such as you are, produce praiseworthy strength for us.

##### English translation
Wise worshippers exalt you, Agni, the bountiful giver of food, the deservedly lauded : bestow upon us excellent strength.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)—The word vipra is read among the names of the wise (Ni. 3-19); it means intelligent people. This word is also used in the sense of the officiating priests who take part in the sacrifice and of the praisers. There is the explanation of Durgācārya: विविधानर्थानस्य प्राप्तुं धातीति विप्रः (*vividhānarthānasya prāptuṃ dhātīti vipraḥ*). Saying that medhāvin means one who possesses intellect, Yāska states the etymology मेधावी कस्मान्मेधया तद्वान् भवति । मेधा मता धीयते । (*medhāvī kasmānmedhayā tadvān bhavati | medhā matā dhīyate |*) (Ni. 3-19). Asking what a medhāvin is, he explains the meaning: the man in whose intellect there is excellent knowledge, a man who possesses such power of intellect.
वाजसातमं सुष्टुतं विप्रा वर्धंति (*vājasātamaṃ suṣṭutaṃ viprā vardhaṃti*)—the people who, praising him with hymns and the like, offer oblations in the sacrifice, this Agni, the giver of food and praised by all, in the sacrifices with fuel and the like

### Printed p. 59
materials, and with oblations of ghee, make to blaze well (to burn brilliantly): such is the purport.
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॑ ने॒मिर॒राइ॑व दे॒वांस्त्वं प॑रि॒भूर॑सि ।  
> आ राध॑श्चि॒त्रमृं॑जसे ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । नेमिः । अरान् इव । देवान् । त्वं । परिभूः । असि ।  
> आ । राधः । चित्रं । यंजसे ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं देवान् परिभूरसि । स्वतेजसा परिभवसि । कथमिव । देमि।
> प्रकाशमानश्चक्रस्य वाह्यो वलयोऽरानिव नाभावर्पितान् सूक्ष्मान् स्थानकानिव :
> किंच त्वं चित्रं नानाविधं राधः । साधयंत्यनेन पुरुषार्थानिति राधो धनं ।
> आ ऋंजसे । स्तोतृभ्यः प्रसाधय । धननामसु पाठाद्राधो धनमित्युक्तं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you surround the gods, that is, you surpass them by your own brilliance. How? Like the shining outer rim of a wheel, which encloses the spokes fixed in the nave, the fine supports, as it were. Moreover you bring about manifold राधः (*rādhaḥ*), riches: that by which men accomplish their aims is riches. Accomplish them for your praisers. Since the word is read among the names of wealth, it is said that riches means wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- नेमिः (*nemiḥ*)—the outer rim of the wheel
- अरानिव (*arāniva*)—as if encircling all the spokes inside the wheel
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- देवान् (*devān*)—all the gods
- परिभूरसि (*paribhūrasi*)—you surround and pervade
- चित्रं (*citraṃ*)—manifold
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—riches
- आ ऋंजसे (*ā ṛṃjase*)—provide (for us)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, as the outer rim of a wheel surrounds all the spokes inside the wheel, you surround and pervade all the gods. May you, who have such greatness, fill us, your praisers, with manifold riches.

##### English translation
Agni, you encompass the gods as the circumference (surrounds) the spokes (of a wheel) : bestow manifold wealth upon us.
### Printed p. 60

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

अग्ने । नेमिः । अरान् इव । देवान् । त्वं । परिभूः । असि । (*agne | nemiḥ | arān iva | devān | tvaṃ | paribhūḥ | asi |*)—As in the wheel of a chariot the outer rim (Rim, or Tyre) surrounds and encloses the spokes (Spokes), Agni stays surrounding all the gods. That is, since he carries the oblation to all the gods, he stays near those gods; the purport is suggested that he goes to all the gods and returns for the carrying of the oblation.
## Sūkta 14 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निं स्तोमेनेति षडृचं चतुर्दशं सूक्तं सुतंभरस्सार्षं गायत्रमाग्नेयं । अग्निं स्तोमेनेत्यनुक्रमणिका ॥ प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोः पूर्वसूक्तेन सहोक्तो विनियोगः ॥
> अनुवादवु—अग्नि स्तोमेन ऎंब ई सूक्तवु मॊदलनॆय अनुवाकदल्लि हदिनाल्कनॆय
> सूक्तवु. इदरल्लि आरु ऋक्कुगळिरुववु. ई सूक्तक्कॆ सुतंभर आत्रेयः ऎंबुवनु ऋषियु ;
> अग्नियु देवतॆयु ; गायत्रीछंदस्सु. अनुक्रमणिकॆयल्लि—अग्निं स्तोमेन ऎंदु मॊदलागि
> हेळिरुवुदु. प्रातरनुवाक मत्तु आश्विनशस्त्रमंत्रपठनक्कागि ई सूक्तद विनियोगविरुवुदॆंदु
> हिंदिन सूक्तद विनियोगदल्लिये हेळलागिदॆ.
> सूक्त—१४
> ॥ मंडल—५ ॥ अनुवाक—१ ॥ सूक्त—१४ ॥
> ॥ अष्टक—४ ॥ अध्याय— १ ॥ वर्ग—६ ॥
> सूक्तदल्लिरुव ऋक्संख्यॆ—६
> ॥ ऋषिः—सुतंभर आत्रेयः ॥
> ॥ देवता—अग्निः ॥
> ॥ छंदः—गायत्रि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The fourteenth sūkta, अग्निं स्तोमेनेति (*agniṃ stomeneti*), has six Ṛks; its seer is Sutaṃbhara, its metre Gāyatrī, its deity Agni. The Anukramaṇikā says: अग्निं स्तोमेनेत्यनुक्रमणिका (*agniṃ stomenetyanukramaṇikā*). Its use at the Prātaranuvāka and in the Āśvina-śastra has been stated together with the preceding sūkta.
### Printed p. 61
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं स्तोमे॑न बोधय समिधा॒नो अम॑र्त्यं ।  
> ह॒व्या दे॒वेषु॑ नो दधत् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । स्तोमेन । बोधय । संइधानः । अमत्यर्णं ।  
> हव्या । देवेषु। नः । दधत् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे स्तोतरमर्त्यममरणधर्माणमग्निं स्तोमेन स्तोत्रेण बोधय ।
> स्वाभिलषितार्थसिद्धये प्रबोधय । योऽग्निः समिधानः स्तोत्रैः सम्यक् प्रकाशमानः स नो हव्यास्मदीयानि हव्यानि हवींषि देवेषु दधत् । निदधातु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O praiser, awaken Agni, the immortal, who has not the nature of dying, with praise, that is, with a hymn; awaken him thoroughly for the accomplishment of your desired aims. May that Agni, who is well kindled by hymns, that is, shines well, place our oblations among the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O praiser) अमूर्त्यं (*amūrtyaṃ*)—who is free from the nature of death
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- स्तोमेन (*stomena*)—with praise
- बोधय (*bodhaya*)—awaken
- समिधानः (*samidhānaḥ*)—Agni, well kindled
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- हव्या (*havyā*)—oblations
- देवेषु (*deveṣu*)—to the gods
- दधत् (*dadhat*)—may he take and place

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praiser, awaken with praise Agni, who is free from the nature of death. May Agni, well kindled, take our oblations and place them among the gods.

##### English translation
Waken the immortal Agni with praise, that, being kindled, he may bear our oblations to the gods.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk, the words "awaken Agni, who is free from the nature of death, with praise and the like" are said with reference to the praise. That is, the sacrificer who performs the sacrifice, addressing the priests such as the Adhvaryu, says "produce Agni by churning, with mantras"; this
### Printed p. 62
meaning is suggested here. How is Agni free from the nature of death? By offering to Agni fuel and oblations such as ghee, he is always shining without going out: this purport is suggested by the word अमर्त्यं (*amartyaṃ*). Since the churning of fire is one of the rites to be performed at the beginning of the sacrifice, the sacrificer is clearly praying to the Adhvaryu that he should produce Agni by churning; to the Hotṛ that he should recite the mantras prescribed for that churning; to the Udgātṛ that he should sing the Sāma mantras such as the Stoma; to the other priests that they should offer in the fire oblations and the like for the gods; and finally to Agni that he should carry the oblations offered to the gods who are intended.
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तम॑ध्व॒रेष्वी॑ळते दे॒वं मर्ता॒ अम॑त्यर्णं ।  
> यजि॑ष्ठं॒ मानु॑षे॒ जने॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । अध्वरेषु । ईळते । देवं । मर्ताः । अनूर्त्यं ।  
> यजिष्ठं । मानुषे । जने ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मर्ता मनुष्या देवं द्योतमानममर्त्यममरणधर्माणं मानुषे
> मनुष्याणां संबंधिनि जने लोके यजिष्ठं यष्टृतमं तमग्निमध्वरेषु सप्ततंतुष्वीळते । स्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Mortals, that is, men, praise at the sacrifices with seven strands that Agni, the shining, immortal one, who has not the nature of dying, the best sacrificer in the world of men, that is, among the people connected with men.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवं (*devaṃ*)—the shining one
- मानुषे (*mānuṣe*)—connected with man
- लोके (*loke*)—in the world
- यजिष्ठं (*yajiṣṭhaṃ*)—the most worshipful
- अमर्त्यं (*amartyaṃ*)—free from the nature of death
- तं (*taṃ*)—Agni
- मर्ताः (*martāḥ*)—men
- अध्वरेषु (*adhvareṣu*)—in the sacrifices
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Men praise in the sacrifices Agni, the shining one, the most worshipful in the human world, who is free from the nature of death.
### Printed p. 63

##### English translation
Mortals worship him, an immortal divinity, at their sacrifices, most adorable among the human race.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

मानुषे लोके अध्वरेषु मर्त्याः (*mānuṣe loke adhvareṣu martyāḥ*) praise: in the world of men, that is, on this earth, in the sacrificial rites proceeding on the sacrificial altar, men who are sacrificers praise Agni with various praises: this is the purport.
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं हि शश्वं॑त॒ ईळ॑ते स्रु॒चा दे॒वं धृ॑त॒श्चता॑ ।  
> अ॒ग्निं ह॒व्याय॒ वोळ्ह॑वे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । हि । शश्वंतः । ईळते । स्रुचा । देवं । धृतश्चता ।  
> अग्निं । हन्याय । वोळ्हवे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शश्वंतो बहवः स्तोतारस्तं देवमग्निमीळते । स्तुवंति । कीदृशास्ते ।
> धृतश्चुता धृतं क्षरंत्या स्रुचा सहिताः । किमर्थं स्तुवंति । हव्याय हव्यवहनार्थं
> चरुपुरोडाशादिलक्षणं हनिर्वोढने वोढुं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The many praisers praise that god Agni. What are they like? They are provided with a ladle that pours ghee. For what purpose do they praise him? For the carrying of the oblation, which has the form of cooked rice, cake and the like, to carry it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- घृतश्चुता (*ghṛtaścutā*)—who pour streams of ghee
- स्रुचा (*srucā*)—provided with the oblation-vessel
- शश्वंतः (*śaśvaṃtaḥ*)—many praisers
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—who is of divine nature
- तं अग्निं (*taṃ agniṃ*)—that same Agni
- हव्याय (*havyāya*)—the oblation
- वोढवे (*voḍhave*)—in order to carry (to the gods)
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Many praisers, provided with the oblation-vessel that pours streams of ghee, praise that same Agni, who is of divine nature, in order to carry the oblation to the gods.
### Printed p. 64

##### English translation
Numerous (worshippers), pouring out butter from the (sacrificial) ladle, glorify him, the divine Agni, that he may convey their oblation (to the gods).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

धृतश्चुता—धृतं क्षरंत्या स्रुचा । (*dhṛtaścutā—dhṛtaṃ kṣaraṃtyā srucā |*) Pouring ghee; that is, when making an offering they fill wooden ladles with ājya (clarified butter) and offer it in the fire. Those ladles are called sruk. Because ghee is poured into the fire from such ladles, they are described by the word धृतश्चुता (*dhṛtaścutā*).
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निर्जा॒तो आ॑रोचत॒ घ्नन्दस्यू॒इज्ज्योति॑षा॒ तमः॑ ।  
> अविं॑द॒द्दा अ॒पः स्वः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । जातः । अरोचत । घन् । दस्यून् । ज्योतिषा । तमः ।  
> अविंदत् । गाः । अपः । स्वरिति स्वः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> जातोऽरण्योर्मथनेन प्रादुर्भूतोऽग्निर्दस्यून् यज्ञविघ्नकारिणः शत्रून्
> घन् हिंसन् ज्योतिषा स्वेन तेजसा तमो घन्नरोचत । अदीप्यत । किंच गा अप
> उदकानि स्वः सूर्यं चाविंदत् । अवेदयत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, born, that is, manifested by the churning of the two fire-sticks, slaying the dasyus, the enemies who obstruct the sacrifice, shone, that is, blazed, destroying the darkness by his own light, his own brilliance. Moreover he found गा (*gā*), the waters, and svar, the sun: he made them known. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- जातः (*jātaḥ*)—as soon as he is born
- दस्यून् (*dasyūn*)—the enemies who cause obstruction to the sacrifice
- घन् (*ghan*)—destroying
- ज्योतिषा (*jyotiṣā*)—by his own radiance
- तमः (*tamaḥ*)—the darkness (driving away)
- आरोचित (*ārocita*)—he shone
- गाः (*gāḥ*)—the cows
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—the waters
- स्वः (*svaḥ*)—the sun (Āditya)
- अविंदत् (*aviṃdat*)—he made manifest

### Printed p. 65

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As soon as he is born, Agni shone, destroying the enemies who cause obstruction to the sacrifice and driving away the darkness by his own radiance. He made the cows, the waters and the sun manifest.

##### English translation
Agni, as soon as born, blazes brightly, destroying the Dasyus, and (dispersing) the darkness by his lustre: he has discovered the cows, the waters, the sun.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the sacrifice, as soon as Agni is brought forth by the act of churning, the darkness is dispelled by his radiance and everything comes to shine with light. The cows, the waters and the sun as well engaged in their respective functions: this is the main import.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निमी॒ळेन्यं॑ क॒विं घृ॒तपृ॑ष्ठं सपर्यत ।  
> वेतु॑ मे शृ॒णव॒द्धवं॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । ईळेन्यं । कविं । घृतपृष्ठं । सपर्यत ।  
> वेतु । मे । शृणवत् । हवं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे जना ईळेन्यं स्तुत्यं कविं क्रांतदर्शिनं धृतपृष्ठं दीपोपरिभागमग्निं
> सपर्यत । पूजयत । सोऽग्निर्मे हवं मदीयमाह्वानं वेतु । कामयतां ।
> शृणवत् । शृणोतु च ॥
> [5]                                                                 [Vol. 19.]

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Addressing the people: worship Agni, who is ईळेन्यं (*īḷenyaṃ*), praiseworthy; कविं (*kaviṃ*), far-seeing (क्रांतदर्शिनं (*krāṃtadarśinaṃ*)); and धृतपृष्ठं (*dhṛtapṛṣṭhaṃ*), he whose upper part of the flame. May that Agni desire (कामयतां (*kāmayatāṃ*)) my invocation, that is, my call, and may he hear it.

### Printed p. 66

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ईळेन्यं (*īḷenyaṃ*)—worthy of praise
- कविं (*kaviṃ*)—far-seeing
- धृतपृष्ठं (*dhṛtapṛṣṭhaṃ*)—having a flame whose base is held (fed) by ghee **NOTE**
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- सपर्यत (*saparyata*)—worship (that same Agni)
- मे (*me*)—my
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—the praise in the form of an invocation
- वेतु (*vetu*)—may he know (and)
- शृणवत् (*śṛṇavat*)—may he listen and hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Worship Agni, who is worthy of praise, far-seeing, and has a flame whose base is held by ghee. May that same Agni know my praise, which takes the form of an invocation, and listen and hear it.

##### English translation
Worship the adorable Agni, the sage, whose summit blazes with butter : may he hear and comprehend my invocation.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

धृतसृष्टं—दीप्तोपरिभागं (*dhṛtasṛṣṭaṃ—dīptoparibhāgaṃ*) **NOTE** Because ghee (ājya, ghṛta) is offered into the fire, Agni is described by the epithet that speaks of ghee at his summit; that is, by the oblations of ghee Agni blazes up well and shines. The meaning is: worship such a resplendent Agni.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं घृ॒तेन॑ वावृधुः॒ स्तोमे॑भिर्वि॒श्वच॑र्षणिं ।  
> स्वा॒धीभि॑र्वच॒स्युभिः॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । घृतेन । ववृधुः । स्तोमेभिः । विश्वचर्षणिं ।  
> सुआधीभिः । वचस्युभिः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋत्विजो विश्वचर्षणिं विश्वस्य द्रष्टारं स्वाधीभिः शोभनाध्यान्यैर्वचस्युभिः
> स्तुतिकामैर्देवैः समेतमग्निं धृतेन स्तोमेभिः स्तोमैः स्तोत्रैर्ववृधुः ।
> अवर्धयन् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The priests (ऋत्विजो (*ṛtvijo*)) augmented Agni, who is विश्वचर्षणिं (*viśvacarṣaṇiṃ*), the beholder of all, and who is accompanied by the gods, who are स्वाधीभिः (*svādhībhiḥ*), of good meditation, and वचस्युभिः (*vacasyubhiḥ*), desirous of praise; they augmented him with ghee and with स्तोमेभिः (*stomebhiḥ*), praises, that is, hymns of praise.

### Printed p. 67

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्वाधीभिः (*svādhībhiḥ*)—worthy of excellent meditation
- वचस्युभिः (*vacasyubhiḥ*)—and desirous of praise, accompanied by the gods
- विश्वचर्षणिं (*viśvacarṣaṇiṃ*)—having the power to see all
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- धृतेन (*dhṛtena*)—with ghee
- स्तोमेभिः (*stomebhiḥ*)—with praises
- ववृधुः (*vavṛdhuḥ*)—they made him grow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The priests made Agni grow with ghee and with praises: Agni, who is accompanied by gods worthy of excellent meditation and desirous of praise, and who has the power to see all.

##### English translation
They have augmented Agni, the beholder of all, with oblation and with praises, along with the gods, the objects of pious meditations, desirous of praise.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the sacrifice the priests caused Agni, who is accompanied by gods worthy of meditation and of praise, and who is able to see everything, to blaze and shine by the oblations of ghee offered with mantras. That is, by the oblations of ghee which the priests offer with mantras, Agni blazed and shone: this is the purport.

## Sūkta 15 (Maṇḍala 5)
The fifteenth sūkta.

द्वितीयेऽनुवाकेऽष्टादश सूक्तानि । तत्र प्र वेधस इति पंचर्चं प्रथमं सूक्तमांगिरसस्य धरुणस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमाग्नेयं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । प्र वेधसे पंचांगिरसो धरुण इति ॥ प्रातरनुवाक आग्नेये क्रत्ौ त्रैष्टुभे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदं सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । प्र वेधसे कवये त्वं नो अग्ने वरुणस्य विद्वान् । आ. ४-१३ । इति ॥ (*dvitīye 'nuvāke 'ṣṭādaśa sūktāni | tatra pra vedhasa iti paṃcarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktamāṃgirasasya dharuṇasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyaṃ | atrānukramaṇikā | pra vedhase paṃcāṃgiraso dharuṇa iti ‖ prātaranuvāka āgneye kratau traiṣṭubhe chaṃdasyāśvinaśastre cedaṃ sūktaṃ | sūtritaṃ ca | pra vedhase kavaye tvaṃ no agne varuṇasya vidvān | ā. 4-13 | iti ‖*)

Translation: In the second anuvāka of the fifth maṇḍala there are eighteen sūktas. Among them the sūkta beginning प्र वेधसे (*pra vedhase*) is the first; it has five ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is धरुण अंगिरसः (*dharuṇa aṃgirasaḥ*); the deity is Agni; the metre is Triṣṭup. In the Anukramaṇikā:

### Printed p. 68
प्र वेधसे पंचांगिरसो धरुणः (*pra vedhase paṃcāṃgiraso dharuṇaḥ*) is said. The sūkta is used in the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka mantras, for the triṣṭup mantras of the Āgneya kratu, and for the recitation of the Āśvina-śastra mantras; this is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra, प्र वेधसे कवये त्वं नो अग्ने वरुणस्य विद्वान् (*pra vedhase kavaye tvaṃ no agne varuṇasya vidvān*) (Ā. 4-13).

Sūkta 15. Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 15; Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 7. Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 5. Seer: धरुण आंगिरसः (*dharuṇa āṃgirasaḥ*). Deity: अग्निः (*agniḥ*). Metre: त्रिष्टुप् (*triṣṭup*).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र वे॒धसे॑ क॒वये॒ वेद्या॑य॒ गिरं॑ भरे य॒शसे॑ पू॒र्व्याय॑ ।  
> घृ॒तप्र॑सत्तो॒ असु॑रः सु॒शेवो॑ रा॒यो ध॒र्ता ध॒रुणो॒ वस्वो॑  ॒
> अग्निः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । वेधसे । कवये । वेद्याय । गिरं । भरे । यशसे । पूर्व्याय ।  
> घृतप्रसत्तः । असुरः । सुशेवः । रायः । धर्ता । धरुणः । वस्वः । अग्निः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> योग्निर्घृतप्रसत्तो हवीरूपेण घृतेन प्रसन्नोऽसुरो बलवान्
> सुशेवः । शोभनं शेवं सुखं यस्यासौ सुशेवः शेवमिति सुखनामसु पाठात् ।
> रायो धनस्य धर्ता पोषको धरुणो हविषां धारको वस्वो वासकोग्निर्भवति
> वेधसे विधात्रे कवये क्रांतदृशे वेद्याय स्तुत्याय ॥ विदिरयं मनिना समानार्थो मनिश्च स्तुतिकर्मा ॥ यशसे यशस्विने पूर्वाय मुख्याय तस्मा अग्नये
> गिरं स्तुतिं प्र भरे । प्रणयानि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Agni who is घृतप्रसत्तो (*ghṛtaprasatto*), gratified by ghee in the form of the oblation; strong; सुशेवः (*suśevaḥ*), one whose शेवं (*śevaṃ*), happiness, is good, since शेव (*śeva*) is read among the names of happiness; रायो (*rāyo*) धर्ता (*dhartā*), the supporter, that is, nourisher, of wealth; धरुणो (*dharuṇo*), the holder of the oblations; and वस्वो (*vasvo*), the bestower of dwelling: that Agni is so. To the वेधसे (*vedhase*), creator; कवये (*kavaye*), far-seeing (क्रांतदृशे (*krāṃtadṛśe*)); वेद्याय (*vedyāya*), worthy of praise (the root विदि (*vidi*) here has the same sense as मनि (*mani*), and मनि (*mani*) denotes the act of praising); to the renowned (यशसे (*yaśase*)), the foremost (पूर्वाय (*pūrvāya*), chief): to that Agni I offer (प्र भरे (*pra bhare*), may I present, प्रणयानि (*praṇayāni*)) praise.

### Printed p. 69

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- घृतप्रसत्तः (*ghṛtaprasattaḥ*)—one gratified by ghee
- असुरः (*asuraḥ*)—the powerful
- सुशेवः (*suśevaḥ*)—endowed with excellent happiness
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—of wealth
- धर्ता (*dhartā*)—the nourisher
- धरुणः (*dharuṇaḥ*)—the one who holds the oblation
- वस्वः (*vasvaḥ*)—the giver of dwelling, he is
- वेधसे (*vedhase*)—the creator
- कवये (*kavaye*)—of far-reaching wisdom
- वेद्याय (*vedyāya*)—worthy of praise
- यशसे (*yaśase*)—possessed of fame
- पूर्वाय्य (*pūrvāyya*)—and foremost; addressing such an Agni
- गिरं (*giraṃ*)—praise
- प्र भरे (*pra bhare*)—I produce well and offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni is one who is gratified by ghee, powerful, endowed with excellent happiness, the nourisher of wealth, the one who holds the oblation and the giver of dwelling. Addressing that Agni, who is the creator, of far-reaching wisdom, worthy of praise, famous and foremost, I compose excellent praise and offer it.

##### English translation
I offer praise to Agni, the creator, the far-seeing, the adorable, the renowned, the first (of the gods); he who is propitiated by oblations, the strong, the giver of happiness, the possessor of riches, the receiver of oblations, the bestower of dwellings.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऋ॒तेन॑ ऋ॒तं ध॒रुणं॑ धारयंत य॒ज्ञस्य॑ शा॒के प॑र॒मे  ॑
> व्योमन्  
> दि॒वो दर्म॑न्ध॒रुणे॑ से॒दुषो॒ न ञ्चा॒तैरजा॑ता अ॒भि ये  
> न॑न॒क्षुः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ऋतेन । ऋतं । धरुणं । धारयंत । यज्ञस्य । शाके । परमे । विओमन् ।  
> दिवः । धर्मन् । धरुणे । सेदुषः । नृन् । जात्यैः । अजातान् । अभि । ये ।  
> ननक्षुः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 70

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये यजमाना दिवो द्युलोकस्य धरुणे धारके धर्मन्यज्ञे सेदुष
> आसीनान् नृनजातान्देवान् जात्यैर्मनुष्यैर्ऋत्विग्निरभि ननक्षुः अभिप्राप्नुवंति ते
> यजमाना यज्ञस्य धरुणं धारकमृतं सत्यरूपमग्निं शाके कर्मणि निमित्ते परम
> उत्कृष्टे व्योमन् स्थान उत्तरवेद्यामृतेन स्तोत्रेण धारयंत । धारयंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificers (yajamānas) who, in the sacrifice that is the धरुणे (*dharuṇe*), upholder, of दिवो (*divo*), the world of heaven, reach (अभि ननक्षुः (*abhi nanakṣuḥ*), approach) together with the born men, the priests, the unborn gods, the leaders, who are seated (सेदुष आसीनान् (*seduṣa āsīnān*)) there: those sacrificers uphold (धारयंत (*dhārayaṃta*) is धारयंति (*dhārayaṃti*)) with ऋतेन (*ṛtena*), praise, Agni, who is the upholder of the sacrifice and of the nature of truth, in the excellent place, the व्योमन् (*vyoman*), namely the uttaravedi, in the rite (शाके (*śāke*), the occasion of the rite).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ये (*ye*)—those sacrificers who
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of heaven
- धरुणे (*dharuṇe*)—which is the support
- धर्मन् (*dharman*)—in the sacrifice
- सेदुषः (*seduṣaḥ*)—those who are seated
- नृन् (*nṛn*)—leaders
- अजातान् (*ajātān*)—and without birth, the gods
- जातैः (*jātaiḥ*)—with the priests who are human beings
- अभि ननक्षुः (*abhi nanakṣuḥ*)—bring together (those sacrificers)
- यज्ञस्य (*yajñasya*)—of the sacrifice
- धरुणं (*dharuṇaṃ*)—the upholder
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—and of the nature of truth, Agni
- शाके (*śāke*)—that which is the occasion of the rite
- परमे (*parame*)—and excellent
- व्योमन् (*vyoman*)—in the place that has the form of the uttaravedi
- ऋतेन (*ṛtena*)—with praise
- धारयंत (*dhārayaṃta*)—have established and made stand

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificers who bring together, with the human priests, the gods who are seated in the sacrifice that upholds the world of heaven, the leaders, the unborn: those sacrificers have established and made stand, with praise, Agni, the upholder of the sacrifice and of the nature of truth, in the place that has the form of the uttaravedi, which is the occasion of the rite and excellent.

##### English translation
They are detaining by sacrifice the true recipient (of offerings) in the most eminent place of the ceremony, who brings together the leaders (of the rite), the unborn, with the born (or gods and men), as seated at the solemnity which is the stay of heaven.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अं॒हो॒युव॑स्त॒न्व॑स्तन्वते॒ व वयो॑ म॒हद्दु॒ष्टरं॑ पू॒र्व्याय॑।  
> स सं॒वतो॒ नव॑जातस्तुतुर्यात्सिं॒हं न क्रु॒द्धम॒भितः॒ परि॑  
> ष्टुः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 71

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अंहःयुवः ' तन्वः । तन्वते । वि । वयः । महत् । दुस्तरं । पूर्वार्य ।  
> सः । संवतः । नवजातः । तुतुर्यात् । सिंहं । न । क्रुद्धं । अभितः । परि । स्थुः॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये यजमानाः पूर्वाय्य मुख्यायास्मा अग्नये महदधिकं दुष्टरं
> राक्षसैर्दुष्प्रापं वयो हवीरूपमन्नं प्रयच्छंति ते यजमानास्तन्वः स्वास्तनूरंहोयुवोऽंहसा वियोजिका वि तन्नते । विस्तारयंति । नवजातः सोग्निः
> संवतः संगतान् शत्रून् तुतुर्यात् । तरतु । किंचाभितः सर्वतो वर्तमानाः
> शत्रवो मां परि वर्जयित्वा स्थुः । तिष्ठेयुः । कथमिव । मृगाः क्रुद्धं सिंहं न
> सिंहमिव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificers who offer to that Agni, the foremost (पूर्वाय्य (*pūrvāyya*), chief), abundant food, वयो (*vayo*), in the form of the oblation, which is great (महदधिकं (*mahadadhikaṃ*), exceeding) and hard for the Rākṣasas to obtain: those sacrificers free their own bodies from sin (तन्वः (*tanvaḥ*) is their own bodies; अंहोयुवः (*aṃhoyuvaḥ*) means separating from sin) and extend them (वि तन्वते (*vi tanvate*), they spread them out). May the newly born Agni overcome (तुतुर्यात् (*tuturyāt*), तरतु (*taratu*)) the enemies assembled (संवतः (*saṃvataḥ*), संगतान् (*saṃgatān*)). Moreover, may the enemies who are all around keep away from me and stand apart, as animals keep away from an angry lion.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ये (*ye*)—which sacrificers
- पूर्वाय (*pūrvāya*)—to the foremost god Agni
- महत् (*mahat*)—abundant
- दुष्टरं (*duṣṭaraṃ*)—and not to be obtained by the Asuras
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food (the oblation) they offer (those sacrificers)
- तन्वः (*tanvaḥ*)—their bodies
- अंहोयुवः (*aṃhoyuvaḥ*)—having made them free of sin
- वि तन्वते (*vi tanvate*)—they cause to grow
- नवजातः (*navajātaḥ*)—newly born
- सः (*saḥ*)—Agni
- संवतः (*saṃvataḥ*)—the enemies gathered round
- तुतुर्यात् (*tuturyāt*)—may he drive far away
- क्रुद्धं (*kruddhaṃ*)—angry
- सिंहं न (*siṃhaṃ na*)—as all the animals, afraid of a lion, stand in very distant regions!
- अभितः (*abhitaḥ*)—the enemies gathered around me!
- परि (*pari*)—leaving me (afraid of me)
- स्थुः (*sthuḥ*)—may they come to stand far away

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificers who offer to the foremost god Agni the oblation that is most abundant and cannot be obtained by the Asuras make their bodies free of sin and cause them to grow. May the newly born Agni drive far away the enemies who have gathered round. As all the animals, afraid of an angry lion, stand in very distant regions, so may all the enemies, afraid at the sight of me, not come near me but stand far away.

##### English translation
They who (present) to the chief (of the gods) the great food (of sacrifice, unattainable {by the Rakshasas), enjoy forms exempt from defect: may that new born Agni scatter assembled (hosts): let them stand from around me, as (deer avoid) an angry lion.

### Printed p. 72
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा॒तेव॒ यद्धर॑से पप्रथा॒नो जनं॑जनं॒ धाय॑से॒ चक्ष॑से च ।  
> वयो॑वयो जरसे॒ यद्दधा॑नः॒ परि॒ त्मना॒ विषु॑रूपो जिगासि॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मात्याइव । यत् । भरसे । पप्रथानः । जनुजनं । धायसे । चक्षसे । च ।  
> वयःऽवयः । जरसे । यत् । दधानः । परि । त्मना । विषुरूपः । जिगासि ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पप्रथानः सर्वत्र प्रथमानो यद्यस्त्वं मातेव जननीव जनं जनं सर्वं
> जनं भरसे बिभर्षि । किंच दायसे धारणाय चक्षसे दर्शनाय च सर्वैः प्रार्थ्यसे।
> यद्यदा दधानो धार्यमाणो भवसि तदा वयो वयः सर्वमन्नं जरसे ।
> जरयसि । अपि च विषुरूपो नानारूपः सन् त्मनात्मना परि जिगासि । सर्वभूतानि
> परिगच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

When you, पप्रथानः (*paprathānaḥ*), spreading everywhere, sustain every man like a mother; and when, for support (धायसे (*dhāyase*), धारणाय (*dhāraṇāya*)) and for seeing (चक्षसे (*cakṣase*), दर्शनाय (*darśanāya*)), you are prayed to by all; and when you are being held (दधानो (*dadhāno*), धार्यमाणो (*dhāryamāṇo*)), then you bring to maturity (जरयसि (*jarayasi*)) all food, वयो वयः (*vayo vayaḥ*). Moreover, being विषुरूपो (*viṣurūpo*), of manifold form, you go round all beings by yourself (त्मनात्मना (*tmanātmanā*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) यत् (*yat*)—when
- पप्रथानः (*paprathānaḥ*)—having gained increase everywhere
- मातेव (*māteva*)—like a mother
- जनंजनं (*janaṃjanaṃ*)—all people
- भरसे (*bharase*)—you bear (when)
- धायसे (*dhāyase*)—for the support of the world
- चक्षसे च (*cakṣase ca*)—and also for looking graciously (you are prayed to, when)
- And यत् (*yat*)—when
- दधानः (*dadhānaḥ*)—being held by all
- वयो वयः (*vayo vayaḥ*)—all food
- चरसे (*carase*)—you cause to ripen (then)
- विषुरूपः (*viṣurūpaḥ*)—possessing forms of many kinds
- त्मना (*tmanā*)—by your own greatness
- परि जिगासि (*pari jigāsi*)—you pervade everything and make it enter into yourself

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, when, having gained increase everywhere, you bear all people like a mother, and are prayed to for the support of the world and for looking on it with a gracious eye, and when, held by all, you cause all food to ripen, then, possessing forms of many kinds, you pervade everything by your own greatness and make it enter into yourself.

### Printed p. 73

##### English translation
When, everywhere predominating, you cherish all men like a mother, and (are implored by all) to behold and to support them : and when, being detained, you mature every kind of food, then, multiform (Agni), you comprise (all beings) in yourself.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वाजो॒ नु ते॒ शव॑सस्पा॒त्वंत॑मु॒रं दोघं॑ ध॒रुणं॑ देव  ॒
> रायः ।  
> प॒दं न ता॒युर्गुहा॒ धधा॑नो म॒हो रा॒ये चि॒तय॒न्नत्रि॑मस्पः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वाजः । नु । ते । शवसः । पातु । अंतं । उरुं । दोघं । धरुणं ! देव । रायः ।  
> पदं । न । तायुः । गुहा । दधानः । महः । राये । चितयन् । अत्रिं । अस्परित्यस्पः॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देव द्योतमानाग्ने उरुं पृथुं दोघं कामानां दोग्धारं रायो
> धनस्य धरुणं धारकं वाजो हविर्लक्षणमन्नं ते त्वदीयस्य शवसो बलस्यांतं
> समाप्तिं संपूर्तिं नु अद्य पातु । रक्षतु । तायुर्न तस्करो यथा गुहा
> गुहायां पदं द्रव्यं दधानो धारयन् रक्षति तद्वत् महो महते राये
> धनाय धनलाभार्थं चितयन् सन्मार्गं प्रकाशयन् अत्रिमस्पः । अत्रिमृषिमप्रीणयः ॥ स्पृ प्रीणनपारणयोरिति धातुः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O shining Agni: may the food in the form of the oblation, which is उरुं (*uruṃ*), vast, दोघं (*doghaṃ*), the yielder of desires, and धरुणं (*dharuṇaṃ*), the supporter, of wealth, guard today the end (अंतं (*aṃtaṃ*), completion, fulfilment) of your strength. As a thief (तायुर् (*tāyur*)) guards an article that he keeps hidden in a cave, so you, for great wealth, that is, for the winning of wealth, showing the good path, gratify the seer Atri.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देव (*deva*)—O Agni, who are of divine nature
- उरुं (*uruṃ*)—abundant
- दोघं (*doghaṃ*)—yielding desires
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—to wealth
- धरुणं (*dharuṇaṃ*)—the supporter
- वाजः (*vājaḥ*)—the food in the form of the oblation
- ते (*te*)—your
- शवसः (*śavasaḥ*)—of strength
- अंतं (*aṃtaṃ*)—the limit
- नु (*nu*)—now

### Printed p. 74
- पातु (*pātu*)—may it protect
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—great
- राये (*rāye*)—to wealth
- चितयन् (*citayan*)—showing us the good path
- गुहा (*guhā*)—in a cave
- पदं (*padaṃ*)—the property placed in secret
- दधानः (*dadhānaḥ*)—having obtained
- तायुः न (*tāyuḥ na*)—as a thief carefully guards it
- अत्रिमस्पः (*atrimaspaḥ*)—carefully protecting Atri, gratify him

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who are of divine nature, may the food in the form of the oblation, which is abundant, yields desires and supports wealth, protect your strength entirely. For the winning of great wealth, showing us the good path, protect Atri carefully and gratify him, as a thief who has obtained property hidden in a cave guards it with care.

##### English translation
Divine Agni, may the (sacrificial) food, the vast yielder (of benefits to men), the sustainer of riches, support the utmost of your vigour ; and do you, considering (the means of acquiring) great riches, like a thief keeping concealed the article (he has stolen), be propitious to Atri.

## Sūkta 16 (Maṇḍala 5)
The sixteenth sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> बृहद्वय इति पंचर्चं द्वितीयं सूक्तमात्रेयस्य पूरोरार्षं । अंत्या
> पंक्तिः शिष्टा अनुष्टुभः । अग्निर्देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं । बृहत्तूरुः पंक्त्यंतं
> हीति ॥ प्रातरनुवाक आग्नेये क्रतावानुष्टुभे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदमादीनि दश
> सूक्तानि चतुर्थनवमवर्जितानि । आदितस्त्रिषु सूक्तेष्वंत्यामुद्धरेत् । तथा च
> सूत्रितं । बृहद्वय इति दशानां चतुर्थनवमे उद्धरेदुत्तमामुत्तमां चादितस्त्रयाणां । अ. ४-१३ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning बृहद्वय (*bṛhadvaya*) has five ṛks and is the second sūkta; its seer is Pūru of the Ātreya family; the last verse is in the paṅkti metre, the rest in anuṣṭubh; the deity is Agni. So it is recorded in the Anukramaṇikā: बृहत्तूरुः पंक्त्यंतं ह (*bṛhattūruḥ paṃktyaṃtaṃ ha*). In the Prātaranuvāka, in the Āgneya kratu, in the anuṣṭubh metre, and in the Āśvina-śastra, these ten sūktas beginning with this one are used, except the fourth and the ninth; and among the first three sūktas the last verse is to be omitted. So it is set out in the sūtra: बृहद्वय इति दशानां चतुर्थनवमे उद्धरेदुत्तमामुत्तमां चादितस्त्रयाणां (*bṛhadvaya iti daśānāṃ caturthanavame uddhareduttamāmuttamāṃ cāditastrayāṇāṃ*) (A. 4-13) **NOTE**

Anuvāda: This sūkta beginning बृहद्वय (*bṛhadvaya*) is the second sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Pūru Ātreya; the deity is Agni; the last ṛk of this sūkta is in paṅkti metre and the other ṛks are in anuṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: बृहत्तूरुः पंक्त्यंतं ह (*bṛhattūruḥ paṃktyaṃtaṃ ha*). The sūkta is used, at the time of the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka mantras, for the recitation of the anuṣṭubh mantras connected with the Āgneya kratu, and for the recitation of the Āśvina-śastra

### Printed p. 75
mantras. Of the ten sūktas following from this sūkta, excluding the fourth and the ninth sūktas, the use is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra, बृहद्वय इति दशानां चतुर्थनवमे उद्धरेदुत्तमामुत्तमां चादितस्त्रयाणां (*bṛhadvaya iti daśānāṃ caturthanavame uddhareduttamāmuttamāṃ cāditastrayāṇāṃ*) (Ā. 4-13). It is laid down that, omitting the last ṛks of this sūkta and of the next two sūktas, since those ṛks are in paṅkti metre, only the remaining mantras are to be recited for the Prātaranuvāka and the Āśvina-śastra mantras.

Sūkta 16. Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 16. Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 8. Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 5. Seer: पूरुरात्रेयः (*pūrurātreyaḥ*). Deity: अग्निः (*agniḥ*). Metre: 1-4 अनुष्टुप् (*anuṣṭup*); 5 पंक्तिः (*paṃktiḥ*).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> बृ॒हद्वयो॒ हि भा॒नवेऽर्चा॑ दे॒वाया॒ग्नये॑ ।  
> यं मि॒त्रं न प्रश॑स्तिभि॒र्मर्ता॑सो दधि॒रे पु॒रः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> बृहत् । वयः । हि । भानवे । अर्च । देवाय । अग्नये ।  
> यं । मित्रं । न । प्रशस्तिभिः । मर्तासः । दधिरे । पुरः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यज्ञे भानवे दीप्तिमतेऽग्नये बृहद्वयो हवीरूपमन्नं दीयते हि ।
> अतस्त्वमपि देवाय द्योतमानायाग्नये वयोऽर्च । प्रयच्छ । मर्तासो
> मनुष्या यमग्निं मित्रं न सखायमिव प्रशस्तिभिः प्रकृष्टाभिः स्तुतिभिः पुरो दधिरे।
> पुरस्कुर्वंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the sacrifice abundant food, बृहद्वयो (*bṛhadvayo*), in the form of the oblation, is given to the radiant (दीप्तिमते (*dīptimate*)) Agni. Therefore you also offer food (अर्च (*arca*), give) to the shining god Agni, whom men set in front, as a friend, with excellent praises (प्रशस्तिभिः (*praśastibhiḥ*), प्रकृष्टाभिः (*prakṛṣṭābhiḥ*)): they place him first.

### Printed p. 76

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Agni
- मर्तासः (*martāsaḥ*)—men
- मित्रं (*mitraṃ*)—as they honour their friend
- प्रशस्तिभिः (*praśastibhiḥ*)—with excellent praises
- पुरः (*puraḥ*)—in front of them
- दधिरे (*dadhire*)—have established (such)
- देवाय (*devāya*)—divine
- भानवे (*bhānave*)—and radiant
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—addressing Agni
- बृहद्वयः (*bṛhadvayaḥ*)—abundant oblation
- अर्च (*arca*)—offer 

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Addressing Agni, who is divine and radiant, whom men have established in front of them, praising him with excellent praises as they honour their friends, offer abundant oblation.

##### English translation
Offer abundant (sacrificial) food to the brilliant divine Agni, whom mortals have placed before them by praises as a friend.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स हि द्युभि॒र्जना॑नां॒ होता॒ दक्ष॑स्य बा॒ह्वोः ।  
> वि ह॒व्यम॒ग्निरा॑नु॒षग्भगो॒ न वार॑मृण्वति ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । हि । द्युभिः । जनानां । होता । दक्षस्य । बाह्वोः ।  
> वि । हव्यं । अग्निः । आनुषक् । भगः । न । वारं । ऋण्वति ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> योग्निर्हव्यं हविर्देवेष्वानुषक् देवेष्वनुषक्तं करोति बाह्वोर्भुयोर्दक्षस्य बलस्य द्युभिर्द्युतिभिर्युक्तः सोग्निर्जनानां यजमानानां होता
> देवानामाह्वाता भवति । भगो न सूर्य इव वारं वरणीयं धनं व्यृण्वति ।
> विशेषेण प्रयच्छति मनुष्येषु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Agni who makes the oblation attached (आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)) among the gods, who is possessed of the splendours (द्युभिर् (*dyubhir*)) of strength in his arms (बाह्वोः (*bāhvoḥ*)), becomes the invoker (होता (*hotā*)) of the gods for the people, that is, the sacrificers. Like Bhaga, that is, like the sun, he bestows especially (व्यृण्वति (*vyṛṇvati*)) desirable wealth (वारं (*vāraṃ*)) upon men.

### Printed p. 77

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स हि अग्निः (*sa hi agniḥ*)—that same god Agni
- बाह्वोः (*bāhvoḥ*)—of his arms
- दक्षस्य (*dakṣasya*)—in the form of strength
- द्युभिः (*dyubhiḥ*)—endowed with splendours
- जनानां (*janānāṃ*)—for the sacrificers
- होता (*hotā*)—being in the form of the invoking priest
- हव्यं (*havyaṃ*)—the oblation
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—he provides (for the gods) in due succession
- भगः न (*bhagaḥ na*)—(and) like the sun
- वारं (*vāraṃ*)—desired wealth
- वि ऋण्वति (*vi ṛṇvati*)—he gives in a special manner

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That same god Agni, endowed with the splendours that are the strength of his arms, and being the invoking priest for the sacrificers, provides the oblation in due order and succession to all the gods. And like the sun he supplies men with the wealth they desire.

##### English translation
For that Agni, (endowed) with the lustre of the strength of his arms, is the ministrant priest of mortals, who (presents) the oblation to the gods in succession, and like Bhaga distributes desirable wealth.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

भगो न-सूर्य इव (*bhago na-sūrya iva*). Like the sun. As the sun is needed by everyone, so wealth too is a thing needed by everyone. The meaning is that everyone desires it.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्य स्तोमे॑ म॒घोनः॑ स॒ख्ये वृ॒द्धशो॑चिषः ।  
> विश्वा॒ यस्मि॑न्नुवि॒ष्वणि॒ सम॒र्यॆ शुष्म॑नाद॒धुः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । स्तोमे । मघोनः । सख्ये । वृद्धशोचिषः ।  
> विश्वा । यस्मिन् । तुविस्वनि । सं । अर्ये । शुष्मं । आदधुः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 78

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मघोनो मघवतो धनवतो वृद्धशोचिषः । वृद्धानि शोचींषि तेजांसि
> यस्यासौ वृद्धशोचिः । तस्यैवंविधस्याग्नेः स्तोमे स्तोत्रे सख्ये च स्याम ।
> तुविष्टणि बहुशब्देर्यॆ स्वामिनि यस्मिन्नग्नौ विश्वा ॥ सुपां सुलुगिति डादेशः ॥
> विश्वे सर्व ऋत्विजः शुष्मं बलं हविर्भिः स्तोत्रैश्च समादधुः सम्यगादधति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May we be in the praise (स्तोमे (*stome*)) and in the friendship of such an Agni: he is मघोनो (*maghono*), the possessor of wealth, and वृद्धशोचिषः (*vṛddhaśociṣaḥ*), one whose splendours, that is, lustres, are great. He is the lord, loud with much sound, in whom all (विश्वे (*viśve*), all the priests) place strength (शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*), बलं (*balaṃ*)) fully, with oblations and with praises.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तुविष्टणि (*tuviṣṭaṇi*)—one possessed of much sound **NOTE**
- अर्ये (*arye*)—and the lord of all
- यस्मिन् (*yasmin*)—in which Agni
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all the priests
- शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*)—strength
- सं आदधुः (*saṃ ādadhuḥ*)—have placed in full: of such
- मघोनः (*maghonaḥ*)—the possessor of wealth
- वृद्धशोचिषः (*vṛddhaśociṣaḥ*)—and of abundant radiance
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Agni
- स्तोमे (*stome*)—in the act of praise
- सख्ये (*sakhye*)—and in friendship may we be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May we be in the act of praise and in the friendship of this Agni, who is the possessor of wealth and of abundant radiance, who has much sound, who is the lord of all, and in whom all the priests have placed strength.

##### English translation
(May we be assiduous) in the praise, (and cherished) in the friendship, of that very radiant possessor of wealth, on whom, loud-sounding and all-ruling, (his) universal (worshippers) have conferred vigour.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

तुविष्टणे बहुशब्दे (*tuviṣṭaṇe bahuśabde*). It is a matter of experience that when a fire is burning it makes a sound such as crackling and so on.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अधा॒ ह्य॑ग्न एषां सु॒वीर्य॑स्य मं॒हना॑ ।  
> तमिद्य॒ह्वं न रोद॑सी॒ परि॒ श्रवो॑ बभूवतुः ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 79

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अध । हि । अग्ने । विषां । सुवीर्यस्य । मंहना ।  
> तं । इत् । यह्वं । न । रोदसी इति । परि । श्रवः । बभूवतुः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने अध अथैषामस्माकं यजमानानां सुवीर्यस्य सर्वैः स्पृहणीयस्य बलस्य मंहना मंहनाय्कॆ दानाय भव ॥ मंहतिर्दानकर्मा ॥ रोदसी
> द्यावापृथिव्यौ यह्वं न महांतं सूर्यमिव श्रवः सर्वैः श्रवणीयं तमित्तमग्निमेव परि बभूवतुः । परिगृह्णीतः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, then (अध (*adha*) is अथ (*atha*)): for us, the sacrificers, be disposed to giving (मंहना (*maṃhanā*) means दानाय (*dānāya*); the root मंहति (*maṃhati*) is read as a verb of giving) of the excellent strength (सुवीर्यस्य (*suvīryasya*)) that is desired by all. Heaven and earth (रोदसी (*rodasī*)) have encompassed (परिगृह्णीतः (*parigṛhṇītaḥ*)) that very Agni, glory (श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)) to be heard by all, as they encompass the great sun (यह्वं न (*yahvaṃ na*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- अध (*adha*)—after our time of worship
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—to us, the sacrificers
- सुवीरस्य (*suvīrasya*)—of excellent strength and wealth
- मंहना (*maṃhanā*)—be disposed to give
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth both
- यह्वं न (*yahvaṃ na*)—as they surround the great sun
- श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)—renowned
- तं इत् (*taṃ it*)—Agni himself
- परि बभूवतुः (*pari babhūvatuḥ*)—stand surrounding

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, after our time of worship, be of gracious mind to give us, the sacrificers, excellent strength and wealth. Heaven and earth both stand surrounding the renowned Agni, as they surround the great sun.

##### English translation
Now verily, Agni, (be disposed) for bestowing excellent strength on these (your worshippers): heaven and earth have invested him with glory like the vast (sun).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू न॒ एहि॒ वार्य॒मग्ने॑ गृणा॒न आ भ॑र ।  
> ये व॒यं ये च॑ सू॒रयः॑ स्व॒स्ति धाम॑हे॒ सचो॒तॆ धि॑  
> पृ॒त्सु नो॑ वृ॒धे ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 80

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सु । नः । आ । इहि । वार्यं । अग्ने । गृणानः । आ । भर ।  
> ये । वयं । ये । च । सूरयः । स्वस्ति । धामहे । सचा । उत । एधि ।  
> पृत् 5सु । नः । वृधे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने गृणानः स्तूयमानः सन् नु क्षिप्रमेहि । यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छ । आगत्य
> च नोऽस्मभ्यं वार्यं वरणेयं धनमा भर । संपादय । ये वयं यजमाना
> ये च सूरयः स्तोतारः सचा हविर्भिः सहिता उभे ये वयं स्वस्ति स्तोत्रं
> धामहे । कुर्मः । उतापि च पृत्सु पृतनासु नोऽस्माकं वृधे समृद्धय एधि ।
> भव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, being praised (गृणानः (*gṛṇānaḥ*), स्तूयमानः (*stūyamānaḥ*)), come quickly to the sacrifice; and having come, bring us desirable wealth (वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*), वरणेयं (*varaṇeyaṃ*) **NOTE**), that is, provide it. We who are the sacrificers and those who are the praisers (सूरयः (*sūrayaḥ*), स्तोतारः (*stotāraḥ*)), both together with oblations (सचा (*sacā*)), offer praise (स्वस्ति (*svasti*), स्तोत्रं (*stotraṃ*); धामहे (*dhāmahe*), we make). And be for our increase and prosperity in the battles (पृत्सु (*pṛtsu*), पृतनासु (*pṛtanāsu*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- गृणानः (*gṛṇānaḥ*)—being praised by us
- नु (*nu*)—at once
- एहि (*ehi*)—come graciously (to our sacrifice)
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*)—the wealth that is desired
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—procure and give
- ये वयं (*ye vayaṃ*)—we who are the sacrificers
- ये च (*ye ca*)—and we who are
- सूरयः (*sūrayaḥ*)—the praisers, we who worship in both ways
- सचा (*sacā*)—together with the oblations
- स्वस्ति (*svasti*)—the praise that goes to meet you
- धामहे (*dhāmahe*)—we offer
- पृत्सु (*pṛtsu*)—in the battles
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- वृधे (*vṛdhe*)—for increase
- एधि (*edhi*)—be gracious

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, praised by us, come to our sacrifice at once. We who are sacrificers and praisers, worshipping you by both kinds of rites, prepared with the oblations, offer the praise that goes to meet you. Be gracious, so that victory and increase may be ours in the battles.

##### English translation
