Glorified by us, Agni, come quickly, and bring us desirable wealth : we who (are your worshippers), we (who are your) adorers, offer you welcome together with oblations: be favourable to us; be (our) success in battles.

### Printed p. 81
## Sūkta 17 (Maṇḍala 5)
The seventeenth sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आ यज्ञैरिति पंचर्चं तृतीयं सूक्तमात्रेयस्य पूरोरार्षमाग्नेयं ।
> अंत्या पंक्तिः शिष्टाः पंक्त्यंतपरिभाषयानुष्टुभः । आ यज्ञैरित्यनुक्रमणिका ॥
> अंत्यावर्जं प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोर्बृहद्वय इत्यादिदशसूक्तमध्ये विनियोग
> उक्तः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning आ यज्ञैः (*ā yajñaiḥ*) has five ṛks and is the third sūkta; its seer is Pūru of the Ātreya family and its deity is Agni. The last verse is in the paṅkti metre; the others, by the convention that the paṅkti comes at the end, are anuṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇikā names it by its first words. Except for the last verse, its use in the Prātaranuvāka and in the Āśvina-śastra has been stated in the use assigned to the ten sūktas beginning with the sūkta before it.

Anuvāda: This sūkta beginning आ यज्ञैः (*ā yajñaiḥ*) is the third sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Pūru Ātreya; the deity is Agni; the last ṛk is in paṅkti metre and the other ṛks are in anuṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is stated as beginning with आ यज्ञैः (*ā yajñaiḥ*). It has been stated, in the use assigned to the previous sūkta, which begins with बृहद्वयः (*bṛhadvayaḥ*), that all the ṛks of this sūkta except the last are to be applied to the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka and Āśvina-śastra mantras.

Sūkta 17. Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 17. Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 9. Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 5. Seer: पूरुरात्रेयः (*pūrurātreyaḥ*). Deity: अग्निः (*agniḥ*). Metre: 1-4 अनुष्टुप् (*anuṣṭup*); 5 पंक्तिः (*paṃktiḥ*).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ य॒ज्ञैर्दे॑व॒ मर्त्य॑ इ॒त्था तव्यां॑समू॒तये॑ ।  
> अ॒ग्निं कृ॒ते स्व॑ध्व॒रे पू॒रुरी॑ळी॒ताव॑से ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

### Printed p. 82

> आ । यज्ञैः । देव । मर्त्यः । इत्था । तव्यांसं। ऊतये ।  
> अग्निं । कृते । सुअध्वरे । पूरुः । ईळीत । अवसे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देव मर्त्यो मनुष्य ऋत्विगित्थेत्थं तव्यांसं स्वतेजोभिः प्रवृद्धमग्निमूतये तर्पणाय यज्ञैः स्तोत्रैरा ह्वयति । किंच पूरुर्मनुष्यः स्तोता
> कृते स्वध्वरे शोभने क्रत्वा तमग्निमवसे रक्षणायेळीत । स्तौति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O god Agni: a mortal, that is, a man, the priest (ऋत्विगित्थेत्थं (*ṛtvigitthetthaṃ*) **NOTE**), thus calls with sacrifices, that is, with praises, for his gratification (ऊतये (*ūtaye*), तर्पणाय (*tarpaṇāya*)) you, Agni, who are तव्यांसं (*tavyāṃsaṃ*), grown strong by your own splendours. Moreover Pūru, a man and a praiser, in the sacrifice well performed (कृते स्वध्वरे (*kṛte svadhvare*)), praises that Agni for protection (अवसे (*avase*), रक्षणाय (*rakṣaṇāya*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देव (*deva*)—O Agni, of divine nature
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—the priest, a human being
- इत्था (*itthā*)—in this manner
- तव्यांसं (*tavyāṃsaṃ*)—you, who have grown by your own splendour
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—for protection
- यज्ञैः (*yajñaiḥ*)—with praises that are parts of the sacrifice
- आ (*ā*) (ह्वयः (*hvayaḥ*))—calls with prayer
- पूरुः (*pūruḥ*)—Pūru, the praiser
- स्वध्वरे (*svadhvare*)—when the excellent sacrifice
- कृते (*kṛte*)—is being performed
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- ईळीत (*īḷīta*)—praises

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, of divine nature, the human priest calls and prays to you, who have grown by your own splendour, for protection, in the prescribed manner, with praises that are parts of the sacrifice. Pūru the praiser, at the time of the performance of an excellent sacrifice, praises Agni for protection.

##### English translation
Divine Agni, a mortal thus with sacrifices (calls upon you) who are endowed with lustre for protection : Puru adores Agni for protection when the sacred rite is solemnized.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

पूरुः (*pūruḥ*). In this ṛk the seer has spoken his own name. Although the verbs of the doer are in the third person, their use in the sense of the first person is a special usage current in the Ṛgveda.

### Printed p. 83
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अस्य॒ हि स्वय॑शस्वरः आ॒सा वि॑धर्म॒ुन्मन्य॑से ।  
> तं नाकं॑ चि॒त्रशो॑चिषं मं॒द्रं प॒रो म॑नी॒षया॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । हि । स्वयशतःतरः । आसा । विधर्मन् । मन्यसे ।  
> तं । साकं । चित्रशोभिसं । मंद्रं । परः । मनीषया ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विधर्मन् । विशिष्टो धर्मो यस्यासौ विधर्मा स्तोता । तस्य संबोधनं । हे स्तोतः स्वयशस्तरः सुयतसां मध्येतिशयेन सुयशास्त्रं मनीषया
> प्रकृष्टबुद्धा साधनेन प्रसिद्धमस्यामुं ह्यग्निमासास्येन वाजा मन्यसे । स्तौषि ।
> किंविशिष्टमग्निं । नाकं सुखं । अकं दुःखं । न विद्यतेकं यस्य सः । तं चित्रतोभिषं चित्रतेजसं मंद्रं स्तुत्यं परः परस्ताश्चितं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa addresses the praiser: "O Vidharman", that is, one whose dharma is distinguished (viśiṣṭa); this is a vocative for the praiser. The praiser is "svayaśastaraḥ", the most renowned among those of good renown. With a mind of excellent understanding, as his means, he praises with his mouth that well-known Agni (the "hi" marks him as famous). Agni is of this kind: "nāka" is happiness; "aka" is pain; he for whom there is no pain is nāka. He is of wonderful lustre (चित्रतोभिषं (*citratobhiṣaṃ*) is glossed as "of wonderful radiance"), adorable (मंद्रं (*maṃdraṃ*) is glossed as "worthy of praise"), and supreme. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विधर्मान् (*vidharmān*)—O praiser, who have faith in various rites
- स्वयशस्तरः (*svayaśastaraḥ*)—you, of exceeding fame
- नाकं (*nākaṃ*)—him who is free from pain
- चित्रशोचिषं (*citraśociṣaṃ*)—who is of manifold splendour
- मंद्रं (*maṃdraṃ*)—who is worthy of praise
- परः (*paraḥ*)—who has a power surpassing worldly condition
- तं अस्य हि (*taṃ asya hi*)—that very Agni
- मनीषया (*manīṣayā*)—endowed with excellent understanding
- आसा (*āsā*)—with speech
- मन्यसे (*manyase*)—you praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praiser, who with faith perform many rites, you, being of exceeding fame, praise with speech endowed with excellent understanding that very Agni, who is free from pain, of manifold splendour, worthy of praise, and of a power surpassing worldly condition.

##### English translation
Performer of various functions, who are deservedly renowned, you praise by your words that Agni, who is possessed of wonderful splendour ; who is exempt from pain ; who is adorable and supreme in understanding.

### Printed p. 84

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

नाकं (*nākaṃ*): अकं दुःखं । न विद तेऽकं यस्य सः (*akaṃ duḥkhaṃ | na vida te 'kaṃ yasya saḥ*). "Aka" means pain; the absence of pain is "nāka", that is, happiness. This is the meaning intended.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्य वासा उ॑ अ॒र्चिषा॒ य आयु॑क्त तु॒जा गि॒रा ।  
> दि॒वो न यस्य॒ रेत॑सा बृ॒हच्छोच॑न्त्य॒र्चयः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । वै । असौ । ऊं इति । अर्चिषा । यः । अयुक्त । तुजा । गिरा ।  
> दिवः । न । यस्य । रेतसा । बृहत् । शोचंति । अर्चयः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> योऽग्निस्तुजा जगद्रक्षणसमर्थेन बलेन गिरा स्तुत्यायुक्त संबद्धो भवॆति
> दिवो न द्योतमानस्यादित्यस्येव यस्याग्ने रेतसा प्रभया कृत्स्नं जगद्वाप्तं
> यस्याग्नेर्बृहद्बृहंतोऽर्चयो दीप्तयः शोचंति प्रकाशंते अस्य वै खल्वग्नेरचिषा प्रभयासावादित्योऽर्चिष्मान्भवति । उ इति पूरणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Agni who is joined with praise (gir) accompanied by "tuja", that is, a strength able to protect the world, is meant here. Like the shining sun, the whole world is pervaded by the radiance (retas) of whichever Agni it is whose great flames (arcayaḥ, "dīptayaḥ") shine forth. By the radiance of this Agni the sun becomes radiant. The word "u" is a mere filler. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- तुजा (*tujā*)—accompanied by the capacity to protect the world
- गिरा (*girā*)—with praise
- अयुक्त (*ayukta*)—joins
- दिवो न (*divo na*)—like the luminous atmosphere
- यस्य (*yasya*)—whose
- रेतसा (*retasā*)—by the radiance
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—of great form
- अर्चयः (*arcayaḥ*)—all the rays
- शोचंति (*śocaṃti*)—shine; of such a kind
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Agni
- अर्चिषा वै (*arciṣā vai*)—by the radiance alone
- असौ (*asau*)—the sun too becomes radiant

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Agni who joins with praise accompanied by the capacity to protect the world, and by whose radiance all the rays, of great form like the luminous atmosphere, shine: by the radiance of that Agni alone the sun too becomes radiant.

### Printed p. 85

##### English translation
He who is endowed with vigour, is (glorified) by praise, and by whose radiance, like that of the sky, the rays of light shine brightly : by his lustre, verily, (the sun is luminous).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk it is described that the sun shines by the radiance of Agni. In many places it is said that Agni shines in heaven (the atmosphere) in the form of the sun, which is one of his three forms. The same idea is indicated here.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्य कृत्वा॒ विचे॑तसो द॒स्मस्य॒ वसु॒ रथ॒ आ ।  
> अधा॒ विश्वा॑सु॒ हव्यो॒ ग्निर्वि॒क्षु प्र श॑स्यते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । क्रत्वा । विचेतसः । दस्मस्य । वसु । रथे । आ ।  
> अध । विश्वासु । हव्यः । अग्निः । विक्षु । प्र । शस्यते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विचेतसः सुमतय ऋत्विजोऽस्य दस्मस्य दर्शनीयस्यास्याग्नेः क्रत्वा कर्मणा
> यज्ञादिना वसु वसूनि धनानि रथ आ दधति । हव्यो यज्ञार्थमाह्वातव्यः सोग्निरथ
> अथ उत्पत्त्यनंतरमेव विश्वासु सर्वासु विक्षु प्रजासु प्र शस्यते । स्तूयते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The vicetasaḥ are the priests of good understanding. By the kratu, that is, by the rite such as the sacrifice, of this Agni, who is "dasma", pleasing to see, they place (ā dadhati) wealth in the chariot. That Agni, who is to be invoked for the sacrifice, is then, immediately after his birth, praised among all the people (vikṣu, "prajāsu").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दस्मस्य (*dasmasya*)—of him who has an attractive form
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Agni
- क्रत्वा (*kratvā*)—as the fruit of the rite of worship
- विचेतसः (*vicetasaḥ*)—the priests of excellent understanding
- वसु (*vasu*)—wealth
- रथे (*rathe*)—in their chariot
- आ (*ā*) (दधति (*dadhati*))—fill
- हव्यः (*havyaḥ*)—who is worthy of the oblation
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- विश्वासु (*viśvāsu*)—all
- विक्षु (*vikṣu*)—by the people
- प्र शस्यते (*pra śasyate*)—is praised

### Printed p. 86

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As the fruit of worshipping this Agni, who has an attractive form, the priests of excellent understanding fill their chariots with wealth. Agni, who is worthy of the oblation, is praised by all the people.

##### English translation
By the worship of him who is pleasing of aspect, the provident (heap) wealth in their cars : Agni, to whom oblations are due, is then grorified by people.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू न॒ इद्धि वार्य॑मा॒सा स॑चंत सू॒रयः॑ ।  
> ऊर्जो॑ नपाद॒भिष्ट॑ये पा॒हि श॒ग्नि स्व॒स्तय॑ उ॒तैधि॑  
> पृ॒त्सु नो॑ वृ॒धे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । नः । इत् । हि । वार्यं । आसा । सचंत । सूरयः ।  
> ऊर्जः । नपात् । अभिष्टये । पाहि । शग्नि । स्वस्तये । उत । ऐधि ।  
> पृत्सु । नः । वृधे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने नोऽस्मभ्यं वार्यं वरणीयं धनं नु क्षिप्रं यच्छ । इद्दीति
> पूरणौ । यतः सूरयः स्तोतारस्त्वत्तः सकाशादासास्येन स्तोत्रेण सचंत धनं
> लभंते । किंच हे ऊर्जो नपादन्नस्य न पातयितर्बलस्य पुत्र वा अभिष्टयेऽभीच्छते मह्यमन्नं प्रयच्छेति शेषः । पाहि । अस्मानापद्भो रक्ष । स्वस्तये
> क्षेमाय पश्वादिलक्षणस्य धनस्य क्षेमार्थं शग्धि । त्वां याचे ॥ व्यत्ययेन मध्यमा॥
> शक्तो भवेति वा। हे अग्ने उतापि च पृत्सु पृतनासु नो मृधे सवृद्धय एधि भव॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Agni, give us quickly (nu) the desirable (vāryam) wealth"; "id" is a filler. For the praisers (sūrayaḥ) obtain wealth from you by their praise (āsā, "āsyena stotreṇa"). Further, O "ūrjo napāt": son of food, or son of strength, that is, "not letting food fall". "Abhiṣṭaye" is for one who desires: the sense to be supplied is "give me food". "Protect us from calamities." "Svastaye": for welfare, for the welfare of wealth such as cattle: "be able" (शग्धि (*śagdhi*)), that is, "I ask you"; the second person is used for the first by interchange. Or it may mean "be able". "And, O Agni, in battles (pṛtsu, "pṛtanāsu") be (edhi, "bhava") for our growth." **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) सूरयः (*sūrayaḥ*)—the praisers
- आसा (*āsā*)—with their praises
- सचंत (*sacaṃta*)—obtain whatever wealth
- वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*)—the same desired wealth
- नः इत् (*naḥ it*)—to us also
- नु (*nu*)—grant quickly
- उर्जो नपात् (*urjo napāt*)—O Agni, son of strength
- अभिष्टये (*abhiṣṭaye*)—to me, who desire food (give food)
- पाहि (*pāhi*)—protect (me)
- स्वस्तये (*svastaye*)—for the securing of our welfare
- शग्धि (*śagdhi*)—be able
- उत (*uta*)—and
- पृत्सु (*pṛtsu*)—in battles
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- वृधे (*vṛdhe*)—for victory and growth
- विधि (*vidhi*)—be a helper **NOTE**

### Printed p. 87

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, grant quickly to us also that same desired wealth which the praisers obtain by their praises. O Agni, son of strength, give food to me, who desire food. Protect me. Be able to secure our welfare. And in battles be a helper for our victory and growth.

##### English translation
Quickly, (Agni, give us) desirable (wealth), such as the devout combining with (pious) praise, (obtain): son of strength, (be favourable) to (my) desires ; protect us (from calamity); be alert for our prosperity ; come for our success in battles.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

ऊर्जोनपात् (*ūrjonapāt*): अन्नस्य पातयितः बलस्य पुत्र ना (*annasya pātayitaḥ balasya putra nā*) **NOTE**. Since the word "ūrjaḥ" has the meanings food and strength, Agni is addressed either as the lord of food, or as the son of strength, that is, one born from the churning done with the use of strength.
शग्गि (*śaggi*): याचे (*yāce*). "I pray." Here the second person singular is used in place of the first person singular. Such usages, and the interchange of case-endings and numbers, are specially employed in the Ṛgveda.

## Sūkta 18 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रातरग्निरिति पंचर्चं चतुर्थं सूक्तं । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका । प्रातर्मृक्तवाहा द्वित इति । मृक्तवाहा इति विशेषणविशिष्ट आत्रेयो द्वित ऋषिः । पंक्त्यंतं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta प्रातरग्निः (*prātaragniḥ*) has five ṛks and is the fourth sūkta (of the second anuvāka). The Anukramaṇikā says: प्रातर्मृक्तवाहा द्वितः (*prātarmṛktavāhā dvitaḥ*). The seer is Dvita Ātreya, distinguished by the epithet "Mṛktavāhā".

### Printed p. 88

> हीति हिशब्दादस्यापि सूक्तस्य पंचमी पंक्तिः । पंक्त्यंतपरिभाषया शिष्टा अनुष्टुभः॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The deity is Agni. The metre goes to the end with a paṅkti (the word "hi" shows that the fifth ṛk of this sūkta is the paṅkti); by the rule that the metre named at the end governs, the remaining ṛks are anuṣṭubh. The application has been stated: the Prātaranuvāka and the Aśvina-śastra.

Anuvāda: The sūkta प्रातरग्निः (*prātaragniḥ*) is the fourth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: प्रातर्मृक्तवाहा द्वितः (*prātarmṛktavāhā dvitaḥ*). The seer of this sūkta is Dvita Ātreya, distinguished by the epithet Mṛktavāhā; the deity is Agni; the last ṛk is in the paṅkti metre; the remaining ṛks are in the anuṣṭubh metre. The application of this sūkta, like that of the preceding sūkta, is for the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka and the Aśvina-śastra mantras.
Sūkta 18
Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 18.
Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 10.
Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 5.
Seer: Dvita Mṛktavāhā Ātreya.
Deity: Agni.
Metre: 1-4, anuṣṭubh; 4, paṅkti. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रा॒तर॒ग्निः पु॑रुप्रि॒यो वि॒शः स्त॑वे॒ताति॑थिः ।  
> विश्वा॑नि॒ यो अम॑र्त्यो ह॒व्या मर्तॆ॑षु॒ रण्य॑ति ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रातः । अग्निः । पुरुप्रियः । विशः । स्तवेत । अतिथिः ।  
> विश्वानि । यः । अमर्त्यः । हव्या । मर्तेषु । रण्यति । १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुरुप्रियो बहुप्रियो विशो यजमाने धनस्य निवेशकोऽतिथिः । यजमानानां गृहान्प्रति तिथिष्वभ्येतीत्यतिथिः । तथाह यास्कः । अतिथिरभ्यतितो गृहान्भवत्य-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"Puru-priya" is "much loved". "Viśaḥ" is the one who establishes wealth in the sacrificer. He is the "atithi" (guest): one who comes to the houses of the sacrificers on the lunar days (tithi). So Yāska says: अतिथिरभ्यतितो गृहान्भवत्य- (*atithirabhyatito gṛhānbhavatya-*) (the passage breaks off at the end of this page and continues in the next window).

### Printed p. 89

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> भ्येति तिथिषु परकुलानीति वा परगृहाणीति वा । नि. ४-५ । इति ॥ एवंविधोऽग्निः
> प्रातः स्तवेत । स्तूयते । अमर्त्योऽमरणधर्मा योऽग्निर्मतेषु मनुष्येषु
> यजमानेषु स्थितानि विश्वानि सर्वाणि हव्या हव्यानि हवींषि रण्यति कामयते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa completes the quotation from Yāska: भ्येति तिथिषु परकुलानीति वा परगृहाणीति वा (*bhyeti tithiṣu parakulānīti vā paragṛhāṇīti vā*) (Ni. 4-5), that is, one who comes on the lunar days to the families of others or to the houses of others. Such an Agni is praised in the morning ("prātaḥ", "stūyate"). The immortal Agni (one whose nature is not to die) desires ("kāmayate") all the oblations that are placed among mortals, that is, among the sacrificers.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अमर्त्यः (*amartyaḥ*)—who is free from the nature of death
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- मर्त्येषु (*martyeṣu*)—that are with the sacrificers
- विश्वानि (*viśvāni*)—all
- हव्या (*havyā*)—oblations
- रण्यति (*raṇyati*)—desires, of such a kind
- पुरुप्रियः (*purupriyaḥ*)—who is wanted by many
- विशः (*viśaḥ*)—for the sacrificers
- अतिथिः (*atithiḥ*)—worthy of worship like a guest
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—at the time of dawn
- स्तवेत (*staveta*)—is praised

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The immortal Agni, who desires all the oblations that are with the sacrificers, who is wanted by many and is worthy of worship for the sacrificers like a guest, is praised at the time of dawn.

##### English translation
Let Agni, the beloved of many, the guest of man, be present at dawn; he who, immortal, desires the oblations of mortals.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द्वि॒ताय॑ मृ॒क्तवा॑हसे॒ स्वस्य॒ दक्ष॑स्य मं॒हना॑ ।  
> इंदुं॒ स ध॑त्त अनु॒षक्को॒ता चि॑त्ते अमर्त्य ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> द्विताय । मृक्तवाहसे । स्वस्य । दक्षस्य । मंहना ।  
> इंदुं । सः । धत्ते । आनुषक् । स्तोता । चित् । ते । अमर्त्य ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने द्विताय द्वितपुत्राय मृक्तवाहसे । मृक्तं शुद्धं हविर्देवेभ्यो
> वहति प्रापयतीति मृक्तवाहाः । तस्मै स्वस्यात्मीयस्य दक्षस्य बलस्य धनस्य ना मुंहना

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Agni, to Dvita", that is, the son of Dvita, who is "mṛktavāhas": one who carries ("vahati", "prāpayati") pure ("mṛkta", "śuddha") oblation to the gods. To him, for your own ("svasya", "ātmīyasya") "dakṣa", that is, strength or wealth, be of a giving mind (मंहना (*maṃhanā*) is explained as "for giving") **NOTE**.

### Printed p. 90

> मंहनाय्कॆ दानाय भव । हे अमर्त्य नित्याग्ने यतः कारणात्ते तुभ्यं स मृक्तवाहा आनुषक् सर्वदानुषक्तमिंदुं सोमं धत्ते धारयति स्तोता चित् स्तोता
> च भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O immortal, eternal Agni": because that Mṛktavāhas, always attached to you, holds the "indu", the Soma, and is also your praiser.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) मृक्तवाहसे (*mṛktavāhase*)—who has pure oblation to offer to you
- द्वितायद्वितनिगॆ (*dvitāyadvitanigè*)—to Dvita
- स्वस्य (*svasya*)—your own
- दक्षस्य (*dakṣasya*)—strength
- मंहना (*maṃhanā*)—be one with a mind to give
- अमर्त्य (*amartya*)—O Agni, free from the nature of death
- सः (*saḥ*)—that Dvita, the Mṛktavāha
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—at all times
- तुभ्यं (*tubhyaṃ*)—to offer, with you in view
- स्तोता चित् (*stotā cit*)—reciting praise
- इंदुं (*iṃduṃ*)—the Soma
- धत्ते (*dhatte*)—holds

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, be of a mind to give your own strength to Dvita, who has pure oblation to offer to you. O Agni, free from the nature of death, that Dvita, the Mṛktavāha, at all times, reciting your praise, holds the Soma in order to offer it to you.

##### English translation
Be (willing to make) a grant of your own strength to Dwita, the bearer of the pure oblations ; for he, immortal Agni, your diligent praiser, brings to you continually the Soma juice.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं वो॑ दी॒र्घायु॑शोचिषं गि॒रा हु॑वे म॒घोनां॑।  
> अरि॑ष्टो॒ येषां॒ रथो॒ व्य॑श्वदाव॒न्नीय॑ते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । वः । दीर्घायुशोचिषं । गिरा । हुवे । मघोनां ।  
> अरिष्टः । येषां । रथः । वि । अश्वदावन् । ईयते ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 91

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने दीर्घायुशोचिषं दीर्घगमनदीप्तिं तं वः । पूजायां बहुवचनं । त्वां मघोनां धनिनां कृते गिरा स्तोत्रेण हुवे । आह्वयामि । हे
> अश्वदावन्नश्वानां दातर्येषां धनिनां रथो युद्धॆष्टरिष्टः शत्रुभिरहिंसितो
> वीयते विगच्छतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Agni, you whose brightness travels far": "vaḥ" is a plural used in honour (of the one addressed). I invoke ("huve", "āhvayāmi") you with praise on behalf of the wealthy ("maghonām"). "O giver of horses": of whichever wealthy men the chariot goes forth ("vīyate", "vigacchatu") unharmed ("ariṣṭaḥ") by enemies in battles.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) दीर्घायुशोचिषं (*dīrghāyuśociṣaṃ*)—who have been shining for many ages
- तं वः (*taṃ vaḥ*)—you, the renowned
- मघोनीं (*maghonīṃ*)—on behalf of the wealthy
- गिरा (*girā*)—with praise
- हुवे (*huve*)—I pray and call
- अश्वदावन् (*aśvadāvan*)—O Agni, giver of horses
- येषां (*yeṣāṃ*)—of those same wealthy men
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—the chariot
- आरिष्टः (*āriṣṭaḥ*)—not harmed by enemies (in battles)
- वि ईयते (*vi īyate*)—may travel happily

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you who have been shining for many ages and are renowned, I praise and call you on behalf of the wealthy. O Agni, giver of horses, may the chariot of those same wealthy men travel happily in battles, not harmed by enemies.

##### English translation
I invoke you bright-shining, through a long life, with praise, (for the benefit) of the affluent, that their chariot, giver of horses, may proceed unimpeded.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> चि॒त्रा ना॒ येषु॒ दीधि॑तिरा॒सन्नु॒क्था पांति॒ ये ।  
> स्त्री॒र्णं ब॒र्हिः स्व॑र्णरे॒ श्रवां॑सि दधिरे॒ परि॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> चित्रा । वा । येषु । दीधितिः । आसन् । उक्षा । पांति । ये ।  
> स्त्रीर्णं । बर्हिः । स्वःऽनरे । श्रवांसि । दधिरे । परि ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 92

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> येष्टृत्वीक्षु चित्रा नानाविधा दीधितिर्यज्ञविषया क्रिया भवति । ये
> आसन्नास्य उक्कोक्कानि स्तोत्राणि पांति रक्षंति तैऋत्विग्भिः स्तीर्णं स्तीर्णस्य बर्हिबर्हिषः पर्युपरि श्रवांस्यन्नानि हवींषि स्वर्णरे । स्वः स्वर्गं नरं यजमानं
> नयतीति स्वर्णरो यज्ञः । तस्मिन्दधिरे । निधीयंते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Among whichever priests there is ("bhavati") varied ("citrā", "nānāvidhā") "dīdhiti", that is, action concerning the sacrifice; who guard in their mouths ("āsan", "āsye") the "ukthāni", that is, the hymns: by those priests, upon the strewn ("stīrṇam") sacred grass ("barhiḥ"), the "śravāṃsi", that is, foods and oblations, are placed ("dadhire", "nidhīyante") in the "svarṇara": "svar" is heaven, and that which leads ("nayati") the sacrificer ("nara") there is the sacrifice, "svarṇara".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- येषु (*yeṣu*)—among whichever priests
- चित्रा (*citrā*)—of many kinds
- दीधितिः (*dīdhitiḥ*)—actions concerning the sacrifice are performed
- ये (*ye*)—who
- आसन् (*āsan*)—in their mouths
- उक्ता (*uktā*)—the hymns
- पांति (*pāṃti*)—guard (by such priests)
- स्तीर्णं (*stīrṇaṃ*)—widely spread
- बर्हिः परि (*barhiḥ pari*)—upon the seats of sacred grass
- श्रवांसि (*śravāṃsi*)—(foods) oblations
- स्वर्णरे (*svarṇare*)—in the sacrifice that leads the sacrificer to heaven
- दधिरे (*dadhire*)—are placed

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Those priests who are renowned for performing sacrificial rites of many kinds, who always recite the hymns from their mouths and so guard them, place the oblations, for the sacrificer, upon the widely spread seats of sacred grass in the sacrifices that lead to heaven.

##### English translation
Amongst whom the ceremonial of many kinds is observed ; who perpetuate the sacred hymns by their recital ; by them the sacrificial viands are placed upon the strewn grass (at the sacrifice which) leads (the worshipper) to heaven.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ये मे॑ पंचा॒शतं॑ द॒दुरश्वा॑नां स॒धस्तु॑ति ।  
> द्यु॒मद॑ग्ने॒ महि॒ श्रवो॑ बृ॒हत्कृ॑धि म॒घोनां॑ नृ॒वद॑मृत नृ॒णां ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 93

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ये । मे । पंचाशतं । ददुः । अश्वानां । सधस्तुति ।  
> द्युमत् । अग्ने । महि । श्रवः । बृहत् । कृधि । मघोनां । नृवत् ।  
> अमृत । नृणां ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये मघवानो दातारो मे मह्यं सधस्तुति स्तुत्या सहितं त्वत्सोत्रसमनंतरमश्वानां पंचाशतं ददुः हे अमृताग्ने त्वं तेषां मघोनां नृणां
> द्युमत् दीप्तिमन्महि महद्बृहत्परिवृढं नृवत्परिचारकमनुष्ययुक्तर श्रवोऽन्नं
> कृधि । कुरु । देहीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The liberal wealthy men who gave me fifty horses together with praise ("sadhastuti"), that is, immediately after the praise of you: "O immortal Agni", for those wealthy men make ("kṛdhi", "kuru"; the meaning is "give") splendid ("dyumat", "dīptimat"), great ("mahi", "mahat"), abundant ("bṛhat", "parivṛḍham") food ("śravaḥ", "annam"), accompanied by men who attend ("nṛvat", "paricārakamanuṣyayukta").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अमृत (*amṛta*)—O Agni, free from death
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ये (*ye*)—those wealthy givers who
- मे (*me*)—to me
- सधस्तुति (*sadhastuti*)—immediately after (praising you on their behalf)
- पंचाशतं (*paṃcāśataṃ*)—fifty
- अश्वानां (*aśvānāṃ*)—horses
- ददुः (*daduḥ*)—gave, of such a kind
- मघोनां (*maghonāṃ*)—wealthy
- नृणां (*nṛṇāṃ*)—for men
- द्युमत् (*dyumat*)—splendid
- महि (*mahi*)—great
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—exceedingly abundant
- नृवत् (*nṛvat*)—accompanied by sons, grandsons and others
- श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)—the wealth of food
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—produce

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O immortal Agni, for those wealthy men on whose behalf I praised you, and who immediately gave me fifty horses, provide wealth of food that is splendid, great, exceedingly abundant and accompanied by sons, grandsons and others.

##### English translation
Immortal Agni, bestow upon those opulent men, who, upon (my) praise (of you), gave to me fifty horscs, and brilliant, ample, and abundant food, (supporting numerous) dependents.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk the seer says that he has praised Agni on behalf of the wealthy, prays Agni to provide them with wealth such as food, and so praises the generosity of the wealthy.

### Printed p. 94
## Sūkta 19 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अभ्यवस्था इति पंचर्चं पंचमं सूक्तं । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका । अभ्यवस्था
> वन्रिर्गायत्र्यावनुष्टुभौ विराड्रूपेति । आत्रेयो वन्रिऋषिः । प्रथमाद्वितीये
> गायत्र्यौ तृतीयाचतुर्थ्यावनुष्टुभौ पंचमी विराड्रूपा एकादशिनस्त्रयोऽष्टकश्च
> विराड्रूपेत्युक्तलक्षणोपेतत्वात् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta अभ्यवस्था (*abhyavasthā*) has five ṛks and is the fifth sūkta. Here the Anukramaṇikā says: अभ्यवस्था वन्रिर्गायत्र्यावनुष्टुभौ विराड्रूपेति (*abhyavasthā vanrirgāyatryāvanuṣṭubhau virāḍrūpeti*). The seer is Vanri Ātreya **NOTE**. The first and second ṛks are gāyatrī, the third and fourth are anuṣṭubh, the fifth is virāḍrūpā, since it has the characteristic stated in एकादशिनस्त्रयोऽष्टकश्च विराड्रूपेत्युक्तलक्षणोपेतत्वात् (*ekādaśinastrayo 'ṣṭakaśca virāḍrūpetyuktalakṣaṇopetatvāt*).

Anuvāda: The sūkta अभ्यवस्थाः (*abhyavasthāḥ*) is the fifth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: अभ्यवस्था वन्विर्गायत्रावनुष्टुभौ विराडूपा (*abhyavasthā vanvirgāyatrāvanuṣṭubhau virāḍūpā*). The seer of this sūkta is Vanvi Ātreya; the deity is Agni; the first two ṛks are in the gāyatrī metre, the third and fourth in the anuṣṭubh metre, the fifth in the metre called virāḍūpā. The characteristic of this metre, virāḍūpā, is stated as: the first three pādas of the ṛk have eleven syllables each and the fourth pāda has eight syllables.
Sūkta 19
Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 19.
Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 11.
Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 5.
Seer: Vavri Ātreya.
Deity: Agni.
Metre: 1, 2, gāyatrī; 3, 4, anuṣṭubh; 5, virāḍrūpā.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भ्य॑व॒स्थाः प्र जा॑यंते॒ प्र व॒व्रेर्व॒व्रिश्चि॑केत ।  
> उ॒पस्थे॑ मा॒तुर्वि च॑ष्टे ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 95

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । अव्यस्थाः । प्र । जायंते । प्र । वव्रेः । वव्रिः । चिकेत ।  
> उपस्थे । मातुः । वि । चष्टे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वन्वेर्खुषेरभ्युत्तरोत्तरमवस्था अशोभना दशाः प्र जायंते । तादृशीर्वनिर्हविषां संभक्ता सोग्निः प्र चिकेत । प्रजानीयात् । ज्ञात्वा चापनयत्विति
> भावः । योऽग्निर्मातुः प्रथिव्या उपस्थे समीपे स्थितं पदार्थजातं वि चष्टे पश्यति॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Of the seer Vanvi (the "vanir"), one after another ("abhi", "uttarottaram") unhappy states ("avasthāḥ", "aśobhanā daśāḥ") arise ("pra jāyante"). Agni, who shares in the oblations, such as he is, is to take full note ("pra ciketa", "prajānīyāt"); the sense is that, having known them, he is to remove them. This is the Agni who sees ("vi caṣṭe", "paśyati") all that exists near ("samīpe") the lap ("upasthe") of the mother, the Earth. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वव्रेः (*vavreḥ*)—to the seer named Vavri (to me) **NOTE**
- अभि (*abhi*)—one after another
- अवस्थाः (*avasthāḥ*)—circumstances of distress
- प्र जायंते (*pra jāyaṃte*)—arise and befall
- (यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni)
- मातुः (*mātuḥ*)—of his mother, the Earth
- उपस्थे (*upasthe*)—sitting in the lap
- वि चष्टे (*vi caṣṭe*)—looks upon everything, of such a kind
- वव्रिः (*vavriḥ*)—Agni, who receives the oblation
- प्र चिकेत (*pra ciketa*)—let him know fully (the distresses of Vavri) and destroy them

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

One after another, circumstances of distress befall the seer named Vavri (me). May Agni, who sitting in the lap of his mother, the Earth, looks upon everything, being the receiver of the oblation, know the distresses of Vavri (mine) and avert them.

##### English translation
Unprosperous circumstances affect Vavri: may the accepter (of oblations) become cognizant (of them), as, reclining on the lap of his mother, he beholds all things.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> जु॒हु॒रे वि चि॒तयं॒तोऽनि॑मिषं नृ॒ण्मं पां॑ति ।  
> आ दृ॒ळ्छां पुरं॑ विविशुः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 96

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> जुहुरे । वि । चितयंतः । अस्यमिषं । नृण्णं । पांति ।  
> आ । द्वळां । पुरं । विविशुः ॥ ३ ॥

**NOTE**

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये वि चितयंतस्तव प्रभावं जानंतो जनास्त्वानिमिषं सर्वदा जुहुरे
> यज्ञार्थं त्वामाह्वयंति आहूय च नृष्णं तव बलं हविर्भिः स्तोत्रैश्च पांति
> रक्षंति ते दृळ्हां शत्रुभिः साधयितुमशक्यां पुरं पुरीमा विविशुः । प्रविशंति । शतं
> पूर्भिरायसीभिर्नि पाहि । ऋग्वे. ७-३-७ । इति निगमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, the people who, knowing ("vi citayantaḥ") your power, invoke you always ("animiṣam", "sarvadā") for the sacrifice, and having invoked you guard your "nṛṣṇam", your strength, with oblations and with praises: they enter ("ā viviśuḥ", "praviśanti") the firm ("dṛḷhām") city that cannot be taken by enemies. As proof, the Nigama: शतं पूर्भिरायसीभिर्नि पाहि (*śataṃ pūrbhirāyasībhirni pāhi*) (Ṛgve. 7-3-7) **NOTE**.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) वि चितयंतः (*vi citayaṃtaḥ*)—people who fully know (your power)
- अनिमिषं (*animiṣaṃ*)—always
- जुहुरे (*juhure*)—call you for the sacrifice
- नृण्मुं (*nṛṇmuṃ*)—your strength
- पांति (*pāṃti*)—protect so as to increase it by hymns and the like
- दृळ्हां (*dṛḷhāṃ*)—that cannot be besieged by enemies
- पुरं (*puraṃ*)—city
- आ विविशुः (*ā viviśuḥ*)—enter

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the people who fully know your power always call you to the sacrifice, performing sacrifice. They protect your strength, increasing it with hymns and the like. They conquer and enter the city that cannot be besieged by enemies.

##### English translation
They who know (your power) invoke you incessantly and nourish (your) strength (by oblations): they dwell in an impregnable city.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ श्वै॑त्रे॒यस्य॑ जं॒तवो॑ द्यु॒मद्व॑र्धंत कृ॒ष्टयः॑ ।  
> नि॒ष्कग्री॑वो बृ॒हदु॑क्थ ए॒ना मध्वा॒ न वा॑ज॒युः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 97

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । श्वैत्तेयस्य जंतवः । द्यु:मत् । वर्धंत । कृष्टयः ।  
> निष्क:ग्रीवः । बृहत् उक्कः । एना । मध्वा । स । वाजयुः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> जायंत इति जंतवः । कृष्णयो मनुष्या ऋत्विजो मध्वा न मधुनेव
> ऐसैनया स्तुत्या । यद्वा नकारश्चार्थः । मधुना च श्वत्रेयस्य । श्शित्रमंतरिक्षं।
> तत्र भवस्य वैद्युतस्याग्नेर्द्युमदिप्तमद्वलमा वर्धंत । अभिवर्धयंति । किंविधाः
> कृस्टयः । निष्कग्रीवो निष्कण सुवर्णेनालंकृतग्रीवाः । व्यत्ययीनैकवचनं । बृह-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"Jantavaḥ" are those who are born ("jāyanta"). "Kṛṣṭayaḥ" are men, the priests. "Madhvā na" is "as with honey", with sweet praise. Or else the particle "na" has the sense of "and", and so "and with honey". "Śvaitreyasya": "śvitra" is the atmosphere; of the Agni who is of lightning, born there, they increase the splendid ("dyumat", "dīptimat") strength. What are the kṛṣṭayaḥ like? "Niṣkagrīvaḥ": those whose necks are adorned with gold, the niṣka; the singular is by interchange. Then "bṛha-". **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- निष्कग्रीवः (*niṣkagrīvaḥ*)—those whose necks are adorned with golden ornaments
- बृहदुक्षः (*bṛhadukṣaḥ*)—those who have abundant hymns
- वाजयुः (*vājayuḥ*)—those desirous of food
- जंतवः (*jaṃtavaḥ*)—those who have been born
- कृष्णयः (*kṛṣṇayaḥ*)—the people, the priests **NOTE**
- मध्वा न (*madhvā na*)—with praises that satisfy like honey
- तैत्रेयस्य (*taitreyasya*)—of Agni who dwells in the atmosphere **NOTE**
- द्युमत् (*dyumat*)—the splendid strength
- आ वर्धंत (*ā vardhaṃta*)—they cause to increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The people, priests and others, who have necks adorned with golden ornaments, who have abundant praise, who desire food, and who have been born, cause to increase, with praises that satisfy like honey, the splendid strength of Agni who dwells in the atmosphere.

##### English translation
Living men, with collars of gold, earnest in praise, desirous of food, augment by this laudation the vigour of you abiding in the white firmament.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रि॒यं दु॒ग्धं न काम्य॒मजा॑मि ज्या॒म्योः सचा॑।  
> घ॒र्मो न वाज॑जठ॒रोऽद॑ब्धः॒ शश्व॑तो॒ दभः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

### Printed p. 98

> प्रियं । दुग्धं । न । काम्यं । अजामि । जानोः । सचा ।  
> घर्मः । न । वाज्‌जठरः । अदब्धः । शश्वतः । दभः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> जाम्योर्द्यावापृथिव्योः सचा सहायभूतोग्निर्दुग्धं न पय इव काम्यं
> कमनीयमजामि दोषरहितं प्रियमस्मदीयं स्तोत्रं शृणोतु । किंविधोग्निः
> घर्मो न प्रवर्ग्य इव वाजजठरः । वाजोन्नं जठरे यस्य सः । घर्मो यथा
> हव्येनाज्येन पयसासिको वाजजठरस्तद्वत् । हविर्जठर इत्यर्थः । अदब्धः शत्रुभिः
> स्वयमहिंसितः शश्वतः शाश्वतो नित्यो दभः शत्रूणां हिंसकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May Agni, the helper ("sacā") of the two kinsfolk ("jāmyoḥ"), heaven and earth, hear our dear praise, which is desirable ("kāmyam", "kamanīyam"), faultless ("ajāmi", "doṣarahitam"), like milk that has been drawn ("dugdham na payaḥ"). Of what kind is this Agni? Like the "gharma", the Pravargya, he is "vājajaṭharaḥ": he in whose belly is "vāja", food; as the gharma is filled with the offering, with ghee and milk, so he is filled with oblation. "Adabdhaḥ" is one not hurt by enemies himself; "śaśvataḥ" is eternal; "dabhaḥ" is the one who harms enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- घर्मो न (*gharmo na*)—as the Pravargya is filled with oblations of many kinds
- वाजजठरः (*vājajaṭharaḥ*)—he who has a belly filled with food
- अदब्धः (*adabdhaḥ*)—he who is not exposed to the harm of enemies
- शश्वतः (*śaśvataḥ*)—for ever
- दभः (*dabhaḥ*)—he who harms enemies, such Agni
- जाम्योः सचा (*jāmyoḥ sacā*)—together with his kinsfolk, heaven and earth
- दुग्धं न (*dugdhaṃ na*)—like milk
- काम्यं (*kāmyaṃ*)—beneficial
- अजामि (*ajāmi*)—faultless
- प्रियं (*priyaṃ*)—this praise of ours; may he hear it

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Agni, who like the Pravargya filled with oblations of many kinds has a belly filled with food, who is not exposed to the harm of enemies and who for ever harms enemies, together with his kinsfolk, heaven and earth, hear this faultless praise of ours, beneficial like milk.

##### English translation
May (Agni) with his two relatives, (heaven and earth), hear this faultless (praise), acceptable as milk : he who like the mixed oblation, is filled with food, and, unsubdued, is ever the subduer of his foes.

### Printed p. 99
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> क्रीळ॑न्नो रश्म॒ आ भु॑वः॒ सं भस्म॑ना वा॒युना॒ वेवि॑दानः ।  
> ता अ॑स्य सन्थृ॒षजो॒ न ति॒ग्माः सुसं॑तिता व॒क्ष्यो॑ वक्षणे॒स्थाः ॥५॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> क्रीळन् । नः । रश्मे । आ । भुवः । सं । भस्मना । वायुना । वेविदानः ।  
> ताः । अस्य । सन् । धृषजः । न । तिग्माः । सुसंतिताः । वक्ष्यः । वक्षणेऽस्थाः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे रश्मे रश्ममन्नग्ने क्रीळन् वनेषु क्रीडन् भस्मना स्वकार्येण भसितेन
> वायुना प्रेरकेण मरुता च सं वेविदानः सम्यक् ज्ञायमानस्त्वं नोऽस्माकमा
> भुवः । अभिमुखो भव । वक्षणेस्था वक्षणे वह्ना स्थिता वक्ष्यः । हनिर्वहंतीति
> वक्ष्यो ज्वालाः ! सुसंशिताः सुतीक्ष्णा धृषजः शत्रूणां धर्षिकास्ता ज्वाला अस्य
> यजमानस्य मम न तिग्मा न तीक्ष्णाः सन् । संतु । अस्तेर्लेटि रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O rays-possessing Agni, playing ("krīḍan") in the forests, with the ashes ("bhasmanā", the burnt remains of your own work) and with Vāyu, the impelling Maruts, well ("saṃ") known ("vevidānaḥ", "samyag jñāyamānaḥ"), be ("ā bhuvaḥ") turned towards us." "Vakṣaṇesthāḥ" are those that stand in the fire ("vahni"); the "vakṣyaḥ" are the flames that carry the oblation. Those well-sharpened ("susaṃśitāḥ", "sutīkṣṇāḥ") flames, "dhṛṣajaḥ", that is, assailants of enemies, are not to be ("na tigmāḥ", "na tīkṣṇāḥ") sharp towards this sacrificer, me ("san" is "santu").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रश्मे (*raśme*)—O Agni, who have rays
- भस्मता (*bhasmatā*)—with your ashes **NOTE**
- क्रीळन् (*krīḷan*)—playing (in the forests)
- वायुना (*vāyunā*)—with the help of the wind
- सं वेविदानः (*saṃ vevidānaḥ*)—you who are visible to all
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ भुवः (*ā bhuvaḥ*)—be turned towards us
- वक्षणेस्थाः (*vakṣaṇesthāḥ*)—which stand in the fire
- सुसंशिताः (*susaṃśitāḥ*)—which are sharp
- धृषजः (*dhṛṣajaḥ*)—which assail enemies
- वक्ष्यः (*vakṣyaḥ*)—which carry the oblation
- ताः (*tāḥ*)—those flames of yours
- अस्य (*asya*)—in regard to this sacrificer
- न तिग्माः सन् (*na tigmāḥ san*)—let them not be sharply burning (let them not harm)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who have rays, you who play in the forest with your ashes and are visible to all with the help of the wind, be turned towards us. May those flames of yours, which stand in the fire, are sharp, are enemy-seeing **NOTE** and carry the oblation, not burn sharply in regard to this sacrificer (let them not harm).

### Printed p. 100

##### English translation
Radiant (Agni), you are made manifest by the wind, and are supporting amidst the ashes (of the forest); be present with us : and may the fierce fiery flames, destructive of foes, be gentle to this your worshipper.

## Sūkta 20 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यमग्न इति चतुर्ऋचं षष्ठं सूक्तमत्रीणां प्रयस्वतामार्षमाग्नेयं ।
> अंत्या पंक्तिः शिष्टा अनुष्टुभः । तथा चानुक्रांतं । यमग्ने चतुष्कं प्रयस्वंतः
> पंक्त्यंतं हेति ॥ प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोरुक्तो विनियोगः ॥ अत्रेश्चतुर्वीराख्येऽहीने द्वितीयेऽहनीदं सूक्तमाज्यशस्त्रं । सूत्रितं च । यमग्ने वाजसातमेति द्वितीयेऽहन्याज्यं । आ. १०-२ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta यमग्न (*yamagna*) has four ṛks and is the sixth sūkta. Its seers are the Atris called Prayasvants; it is ārṣa and Āgneya. The last ṛk is paṅkti and the rest are anuṣṭubh. So it is recorded in the Anukramaṇikā: यमग्ने चतुष्कं प्रयस्वंतः पंक्त्यंतं हेति (*yamagne catuṣkaṃ prayasvaṃtaḥ paṃktyaṃtaṃ heti*). The application for the Prātaranuvāka and the Aśvina-śastra has been stated. In the ahīna called Caturvīra of Atri, on the second day, this sūkta is the Ājya-śastra. It is also laid down in the sūtra यमग्ने वाजसातमेति द्वितीयेऽहन्याज्यं (*yamagne vājasātameti dvitīye 'hanyājyaṃ*) (Ā. 10-2).

Anuvāda: The sūkta यमग्ने (*yamagne*) is the sixth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has four ṛks. The seers of this sūkta are the Atris called Prayasvants; the deity is Agni; the last ṛk is in the paṅkti metre and the remaining ṛks are in the anuṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: यमग्ने चतुष्कं प्रयस्वंतः पंक्त्यंतं हि (*yamagne catuṣkaṃ prayasvaṃtaḥ paṃktyaṃtaṃ hi*). The application of this sūkta is for the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka and the Aśvina-śastra mantras. In the Caturvīra ahīna sacrifice performed by the Atri seers, this sūkta is to be used on the second day for the recitation of the Ājya-śastra mantras, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra, यमग्ने वाजसातमेति द्वितीयेऽहन्याज्यं (*yamagne vājasātameti dvitīye 'hanyājyaṃ*) (Ā. 10-2).
Sūkta 20
Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 20.
Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 12.
Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 4.
Seers: the Atris called Prayasvants.
Deity: Agni.
Metre: 1-3, anuṣṭubh; 4, paṅkti.

### Printed p. 101
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यम॑ग्ने वाजसातम॒ त्वं चि॒न्म्य॑से र॒यिं ।  
> तं नो॑ गी॒र्भिः श्र॒वाय्यं॑ देव॒त्रा प॑नया॒ युजं॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यं । अग्ने । वाजसातम । त्वं । चित् । मन्यसे । रयिं ।  
> तं । नः । गीभिः । श्रवाय्यं । देवत्रा । पनय । युजं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने हे वाजसातमात्यंतमन्नद त्वं यं रयिं धनमस्माभिर्दीयमानं
> हवीरूपं धनं मन्यसे बुध्यसे । चिदिति पादपूरणः । श्रवाय्यं श्रवणीयं शस्यं
> गीर्भिः स्तुतिभिर्युजं युक्तं नोऽस्मदीयं हविर्लक्षणं धनं तं देवत्रा देवेषु
> पनय । प्रापयेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Agni, O Vājasātama", O one who gives food in the highest degree: whatever wealth ("rayim") in the form of oblation, given by us, you approve ("manyase", "budhyase"); ("cit" is a mere filler) that wealth of ours, in the form of oblation, which is worthy to be heard ("śravāyyam", "śravaṇīyam", "śasyam") and is joined ("yujam", "yuktam") with praises ("gīrbhiḥ"), convey ("panaya", "prāpaya") to the gods ("devatrā").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वाजसातम (*vājasātama*)—who give food in abundance
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- यं रयिं (*yaṃ rayiṃ*)—which wealth, in the form of our oblation
- मन्यसे (*manyase*)—you approve, of such a kind
- श्रवाय्यं (*śravāyyaṃ*)—which is worthy of praise
- गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)—with praises
- युजं (*yujaṃ*)—which is joined
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- तं (*taṃ*)—that same oblation
- देवत्रा (*devatrā*)—to the gods
- पनय (*panaya*)—convey

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who give food in abundance, convey to the gods that same oblation of ours, praiseworthy and joined with praises, which is the wealth in the form of oblation that you approve.

##### English translation
That (sacrificial) wealth, Agni, bounteous giver of food, of which you approve, and which deserves to be commended by our praises, convey to the gods

### Printed p. 102

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ये अ॑ग्ने॒ नेरयं॑ति ते वृ॒द्धा उ॒ग्रस्य॒ शव॑सः ।  
> अप॒ द्वेषो॒ अप॒ ह्वरो॒ऽन्यव्र॑तस्य सच्चिरे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ये । अग्ने । न । ईरयंति । ते । वृद्धाः । उग्रस्य । शवसः ।  
> अप । द्वेषः । अप । ह्वरः । अन्यव्रतस्य । सश्चिरे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने ये जना वृद्धाः पश्वादिलक्षणैर्धनैः समृद्धाः संतस्ते तुभ्यं
> नेरयंति हवींषि न प्रयच्छंति त उग्रस्याधिकेन शवसो बलेनान्नेन वाप नीताः ।
> बलान्नहीना भवंतीत्यर्थः । किंचान्यव्रतस्यान्यद्वैदिकाद्विभक्तं व्रतं कर्म यस्य
> तस्यासुरस्य द्वेषस्त्वत्संबंधिनं विरोधं ह्वरोऽतिहिंसां चाप सच्चिरे । आत्मानं
> प्रापयंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, those men who are prosperous with wealth in the form of cattle and the like, yet do not offer you oblations, are deprived of strength or food, and so become destitute of strength and food. Further, the Asura whose observance (vrata, rite) is other than the Vedic, set apart from it, incurs your enmity and your violence, that is, severe punishment; they bring these upon themselves.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ये (*ye*)—those men who
- वृद्धाः (*vṛddhāḥ*)—even though they have attained increase of wealth
- ते (*te*)—to you
- नेरयंति (*nerayaṃti*)—do not offer oblations, they
- उग्रस्य (*ugrasya*)—great
- शवसः (*śavasaḥ*)—from strength
- अपॆ (*apè*)—become destitute
- अन्यव्रतस्य (*anyavratasya*)—those who, leaving the Vedic rite, are performers of non-Vedic rites
- द्वेषः (*dveṣaḥ*)—your enmity
- ह्वरः (*hvaraḥ*)—(from you) violence
- अप सच्चिरे (*apa saccire*)—they receive

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, those men who do not offer you oblations, even though they have attained increase of wealth, become destitute of great strength. And men who have left the Vedic rite and fallen from their duty receive your enmity and violence from you.

##### English translation
May those prosperous men who do not offer oblations to you become destitute of great strength, and may (the followers) of other (than Vaidik) observances incur (your) enmity and punishment.

### Printed p. 103
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> होता॑रं त्वा वृणीम॒हेऽग्ने॒ दक्ष॑स्य॒ साध॑नं ।  
> य॒ज्ञेषु॑ पू॒र्व्यं गि॒रा प्रय॑स्वंतो हवामहे ॥३॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> होतारं । त्वा । वृणीमहे । अग्ने । दक्षस्य । साधनं ।  
> यज्ञेषु । पूर्व्यं । गिरा । प्रयस्वंतः । हनामहे ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने प्रयस्वंतोऽन्नवंत एतन्नामका वयं दक्षस्य बलस्य साधनं साधकं
> साधयितारं त्वां होतारं देवानामाह्वातारं वृणीमहे । किंच यज्ञेषु
> पूर्व्यं मुख्यं त्वां गिरा स्तुतिरूपया वाचा हवामहे । स्तुमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, we who are called Prayasvats, that is, possessors of food, choose you as hotṛ, the summoner of the gods, you who are the accomplisher, the one who brings about, of strength. Moreover, you who are foremost, the chief, at sacrifices, we call upon with speech in the form of praise; that is, we praise you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- प्रयस्वंतः (*prayasvaṃtaḥ*)—we who are called Prayasvats
- दक्षस्य (*dakṣasya*)—of strength
- साधनं त्वा (*sādhanaṃ tvā*)—you, the accomplisher
- होतारं (*hotāraṃ*)—as the one who calls the gods to the sacrifice (as hotṛ)
- वृणीमहे (*vṛṇīmahe*)—we choose
- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—at sacrifices
- पूर्व्यं (*pūrvyaṃ*)—you, the chief
- गिरा (*girā*)—with words of praise
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we pray and call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, we who are called Prayasvats choose you, the accomplisher of strength, as hotṛ. We pray to you, the chief at the sacrifice, and call upon you with words of praise.

##### English translation
We, Prayasvats, have recourse to you, the invoker (of the gods), the means of strength : we glorify you first at sacrifices with praise.

### Printed p. 104
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒त्था यथा॑ त ऊ॒तये॒ सह॑सावन्दि॒वेदि॑वे ।  
> रा॒य ऋ॒ताय॑ सुक्रतो॒ गोभिः॑ स्याम सध॒मादो॑  
> वी॒रैः स्या॑म सध॒मादः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इत्था । यथा । ते । ऊतये । सहसावन् । दिवेऽदिवे ।  
> राये । ऋताय । सुकृतो इति सुकृतो । गोभिः । स्याम । सध्वमादः ।  
> वीरैः । स्याम । सधमादः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सहसावन् बलवन्नग्ने दिवे दिवेऽन्वहं वयं ते तवोतये रक्षणाय
> यथा स्याम तथेत्थेत्थं कुरु । हे सुक्रतो राये धनाय ऋताय यज्ञाय च
> यथा स्याम तथा कुरु । किंच वयु गोभिः सधमादः सह माद्यंतः स्याम । वीरैः
> पुत्रैश्च सधमादः सह माद्यंतः स्याम । तथा कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, possessor of strength, day after day do so (ittha), act in such a way that we may be in your protection. O doer of good deeds, act so that we may be (devoted) to wealth and to the sacrifice. Moreover, may we rejoice together with cattle, and rejoice together with sons, who are heroes (vīra); act so.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सहसावन् (*sahasāvan*)—O Agni, endowed with strength
- दिवेदिवे (*divedive*)—every day
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—(we)
- ते (*te*)—your
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—in protection
- यथा (*yathā*)—in whatever manner it should be
- इत्था (*itthā*)—so favour us
- सुक्रतो (*sukrato*)—O Agni, performer of excellent works
- राये (*rāye*)—for the gain of wealth
- ऋताय (*ṛtāya*)—intent on sacrificial rites
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with cattle
- सधमादः (*sadhamādaḥ*)—rejoicing together
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be
- वीरैः (*vīraiḥ*)—with heroic sons
- सदमादः (*sadamādaḥ*)—let us be rejoicing together

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, endowed with strength, grant that we may be in your protection every day. O Agni, performer of excellent works, grant that we may be intent on sacrificial rites for the gain of wealth, and as the result of it may rejoice together with cattle and with heroic sons.

### Printed p. 105

##### English translation
Possessor of strength, day by day so (provide), that we may enjoy your protection; doer of good deeds, may we (be deserving) of wealth through sacrifice, and may we be happy with cattle, happy with male descendants.

## Sūkta 21 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-first sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मनुष्वत्वेति चतुर्यचं सप्तमं सूक्तमात्रेयस्य ससस्यार्षं । मनुष्वत्सस
> इत्यनुक्रमणिका । पंक्त्यंतं हेति पूर्वत्रोक्तत्वादिदमादीनि त्रीणि सूक्तानि
> पंक्त्यंतानि । शिष्टा अनुष्टुभः । अग्निर्देवता ॥ प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोरुक्तो
> विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning "Manuṣvat" has four Ṛks and is the seventh sūkta (of the second anuvāka). Its seer is Sasa Ātreya; the Anukramaṇikā says "Manuṣvat…". **NOTE** Since it was stated earlier (in the Anukramaṇikā) that the verse is "paṅkty-antam", the three sūktas beginning with this one end in a paṅkti verse; the remaining verses are anuṣṭubh. The deity is Agni. Its use (viniyoga) in the Prātaranuvāka and in the Āśvina-śastra has been stated.

Translation (anuvāda): This sūkta beginning "Manuṣvat" is the seventh sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has four Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Sasa Ātreya; Agni is the deity. Since the Anukramaṇikā says "paṅkty-antaṃ ha", the last Ṛk of this sūkta and of the following three sūktas is in the paṅkti metre; the remaining Ṛks are in the anuṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said "manuṣyat-sasaḥ". This sūkta is used for the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka and the mantras of the Āśvina-śastra.

Sūkta 21
Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 21
Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 13
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4
Ṛṣi: Sasa Ātreya
Devatā: Agni
Chandas: 1–3 Anuṣṭubh; 4 Paṅkti

### Printed p. 106
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒नु॒ष्वत्वा॒ नि धी॑महि मनु॒ष्वत्समि॑धीमहि ।  
> अग्ने॑ मनु॒ष्पदं॑गिरो दे॒वान्दे॑वय॒ते य॑ज ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मनुष्वत् ' त्वा । नि । धीमहि । मनुष्वत् । सं । इधीमहि ।  
> अग्ने मनुष्वत् । अंगिरः । देवान् । देवयते । यज ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वा त्वां मनुष्वन्मनुरिव नि धीमहि । मनुःशब्दो मनुपर्यायः॥
> नभोंगिरोमनुषां वत्युपसंख्यानमिति भसंज्ञयां रुत्वाभावः॥ मनुष्वन्मनुरिव
> त्वा समिधीमहि । समिद्धिः संदीपयामः । हे अंगिरोंगारात्मकाग्ने देवयते
> देवकामाय यजमानाय मनुष्वन्मनवे मनोरपत्याय देवान् यज ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, we meditate upon you as Manu did (manuṣvat), like Manu. Like Manu we kindle you; that is, we cause you to blaze with kindling-sticks. O Agni, Aṅgiras, of the nature of coals (aṅgāra), worship the gods for the sacrificer who desires the gods, as for Manu, the descendant of Manu.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- मनुष्वत् (*manuṣvat*)—like Manu
- नि धीमहि (*ni dhīmahi*)—we meditate
- मनुष्वत् (*manuṣvat*)—as Manu did
- समिधीमहि (*samidhīmahi*)—we cause (you) to blaze
- अंगिरः (*aṃgiraḥ*)—O Aṅgiras, of the nature of coals
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- देवयते (*devayate*)—on behalf of the sacrificer who desires the gods as helpers
- मनुष्वत् (*manuṣvat*)—like Manu
- देवान् (*devān*)—addressing the gods
- यज (*yaja*)—perform the sacrifice

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, as Manu meditated upon you, so do we also meditate upon you. As Manu caused you to blaze, so do we also. O Agni, of the nature of coals, for the sacrificer who desires to win the favour of the gods, do you also, like Manu, perform the sacrifice addressing the gods.

##### English translation
Like Manu, we mediate, Agni, upon you ; like Manu, we kindled you : worship the gods on behalf of the (worshipper), devout as Manu.

### Printed p. 107
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं हि मानु॑षे॒ जनेऽग्ने॒ सुप्री॑त इ॒ध्यसे॑ ।  
> स्रुच॑स्त्वा यंत्यानु॒षक्सुजा॑त॒ सर्पि॑रासुते ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । हि । मानुषे । जने । अग्ने । सुप्रीतः । इध्यसे ।  
> स्रुचः । त्वा । यंति । आनुषक् । सुजात । सर्पिःआसुते ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं मानुषे जने मनुष्यलोके सुप्रीतः स्तोत्रैः सुष्टु प्रीतः
> सन् इध्यसे । दीप्यसे । स्रुचो हविर्भिः संपूर्णा आनुषक् अनुषक्तं यथा भवति
> तथा त्वा त्वां यंति । प्राप्नुवंति । हे सुजात हे सर्पिरासुते धृतयुक्तान्नेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you who are well pleased with praises in the human world (among the human race) are kindled, that is, you shine. The ladles, filled with oblations, come to you, reach you, constantly (ānuṣak, without break). O well-born one, O Agni, to whom clarified butter is poured, possessed of food mixed with ghee.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- सुप्रीतः (*suprītaḥ*)—when you are well satisfied with praises and the like
- मानुषे जने (*mānuṣe jane*)—in the assembly of men
- इध्यसे (*idhyase*)—you blaze forth
- सुजात (*sujāta*)—you of excellent birth
- सर्पिरासुते (*sarpirāsute*)—you who have food in the form of ghee, O Agni
- स्रुचः (*srucaḥ*)—the ladle-vessels filled with oblations
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—continually
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- यंति (*yaṃti*)—approach

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, when you are satisfied with praises and the like, you blaze forth in the assembly of men. O Agni, of excellent birth and having food in the form of ghee, the ladles filled with oblations continually approach you.

##### English translation
When you are pleased, Agni, you shine upon the human race; well-born, feeder upon clarified butter, the ladles are constantly uplifted to you.

### Printed p. 108
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वां विश्वे॑ स॒जोष॑सो दे॒वासो॑ दू॒तम॑क्रत ।  
> स॒प॒र्यंत॑स्त्वा कवे य॒ज्ञेषु॑ दे॒वमी॑ळते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वां । विश्वे । सजोषसः । देवासः । दूतं । अक्रत ।  
> सपर्यंतः । त्वा । कवे । यज्ञेषु । देवं । ईळते ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सजोषसः सह प्रीयमाणा विश्वे सर्वॆ देवासो देवास्त्वां दूतमक्रत ।
> अकुर्वन् ! अतः कारणात् हे कवे क्रांतदर्शिन् देवं द्योतमानं त्वा त्वां
> सपर्यंतः परिचरंतो यजमाना यज्ञॆष्वीळते । देवानाह्वातुं याचंते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

All the gods, being pleased together (sajoṣasaḥ), have made you their messenger (dūta). For this reason, O Kavi, far-seeing one, the sacrificers, serving you who are shining (deva), at the sacrifices praise you; that is, they beseech you in order to summon the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सजोषसः (*sajoṣasaḥ*)—having equal affection for you
- विश्वे (*viśve*)—all
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—all the gods
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- दूतं (*dūtaṃ*)—as messenger
- अक्रत (*akrata*)—have made (therefore)
- कवे (*kave*)—O Agni, far-seeing one
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—shining
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—at sacrifices
- सपर्यंतः (*saparyaṃtaḥ*)—the sacrificers who worship
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wise Agni, all the gods, who have equal affection for you, have chosen you as their messenger. Therefore the sacrificers, worshipping at the sacrifices, praise you, who are divine in nature and shining.

##### English translation
All the consenting divinities have made you their messenger ; therefore the pious worship you who are divine, wise Agni, at sacrifices.

### Printed p. 109
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दे॒वं वो॑ देवय॒ज्यया॒ग्निमी॑ळीत॒ मर्त्यः॑ ।  
> समि॑द्धः शुक्र दीदिह्यृ॒तस्य॒ योनि॒मस॑दः स॒सस्य॒ योनि॒मास॑दः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> देवं । वः । देव्ययज्यया । अग्निं । ईळीत । मर्त्यः ।  
> संइद्धः । शुक्र । दीदिहि । ऋतस्य । योनिं । आ । असदः । ससस्य । योनिं।  
> आ । असदः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मर्त्यो मनुष्यो देवं द्योतमानमग्नि मग्रण्यं वस्तां देवयज्यया
> देवयज्यायै देवयागार्थमीळीत । स्तोतुमर्हति । हे शुक्र शोचिष्मन्
> समिद्धो हविर्भिः प्रवृद्धः सन् दीदिहि । दीप्यस्व । ऋतस्य सत्यभूतस्य ससस्य ऋषेर्मनु योनिं योनिकारणं स्वर्गसाधनं यज्ञस्थानमासदः । देवतारूपेणासीद ।
> आदरातिशयद्योतनार्थं पुनर्वचनं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The mortal, the man, is fit to praise the shining Agni, the foremost, for the sake of the worship of the gods (devayajyā), that is, in order to sacrifice to the gods. O bright (śukra) Agni, possessed of splendour, kindled, grown strong with oblations, blaze. Of Sasa, the seer, who is truthful (ṛta), take your seat in the place of origin (yoni), the cause of origin, the means of reaching heaven, the place of sacrifice; sit there in the form of a deity. **NOTE** The repetition (of "āsadaḥ") is to show great respect.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—man
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—shining
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—and of the nature of Agni
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- देवयज्यया (*devayajyayā*)—in order to perform the sacrifice addressed to the gods
- ईळीत (*īḷīta*)—praises
- शुक्र (*śukra*)—O Agni, endowed with radiance
- समिद्धः (*samiddhaḥ*)—when you are kindled with oblations and the like
- दीदिहि (*dīdihi*)—shine
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the follower of truth
- ससस्य (*sasasya*)—of the seer named Sasa
- योनिं (*yoniṃ*)—in the place of sacrifice
- आसदः (*āsadaḥ*)—be seated
- Or else: ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice; योनिं (*yoniṃ*)—in the place of origin; आसदः (*āsadaḥ*)—be seated; ससस्य (*sasasya*)—of the seer named Sasa; योनिं (*yoniṃ*)—in the place of sacrifice; आसदः (*āsadaḥ*)—be seated.

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, a man praises you, who are shining and divine in nature, in order to perform the sacrifice addressed to the gods. O Agni, endowed with radiance, kindled with oblations and the like, shine. Be seated in the place of sacrifice, true in nature, of the seer named Sasa, the follower of truth.

### Printed p. 110

##### English translation
Man praises you, divine Agni, to convey his offerings to the Gods; bright Agni, blaze when kindled : take your seat in the chamber (of sacrifice): in the chamber of the sincere Sasa.

## Sūkta 22 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-second sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्र विश्वसामन्निति चतुर्ऋचमष्टमं सूक्तं । आत्रेयो विश्वसामा ऋषिः ।
> चतुर्थी पंक्तिः शिष्टा अनुष्टुभः । अग्निर्देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं । प्र विश्वसाम
> न्विश्वसामेति ॥ उक्तो विनियोगः प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning "Pra viśvasāman" has four Ṛks and is the eighth sūkta. The seer is Ātreya Viśvasāman. The fourth verse is paṅkti, the rest are anuṣṭubh. The deity is Agni. So it is stated in the Anukramaṇikā ("Pra viśvasāman viśvasāma"). Its use (viniyoga) in the Prātaranuvāka and in the Āśvina-śastra has been stated.

Translation (anuvāda): This sūkta beginning "Pra viśvasāman" is the eighth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has four Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Viśvasāmā Ātreya; Agni is the deity; the fourth Ṛk is in the paṅkti metre. The remaining Ṛks are in the anuṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said "Pra viśvasāman viśvasāma". This sūkta is used for the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka and the mantras of the Āśvina-śastra.

Sūkta 22
Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 22
Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 14
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4
Ṛṣi: Viśvasāmā Ātreya
Devatā: Agni
Chandas: 1–3 Anuṣṭubh; 4 Paṅkti

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र वि॑श्वसामन्नत्रि॒वदर्चा॑ पाव॒कशो॑चिषे ।  
> यो अ॑ध्व॒रेस्वीड्यो॒ होता॑ मं॒द्रत॑मो वि॒ति ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 111

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । विश्वसामन् । अत्रिवत् । अर्च । पावक्यशोचिषे ।  
> यः । अध्वरेषु । ईड्यः । होता । मद्रतमः । विशि ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विश्वसामन् । ऋषेः संबोधनमेतत् । त्वमत्रिवदत्रिरिव पावकशोचिषे
> शोधकदीप्तये तस्मा अग्नये प्रार्च । प्रगाय । योऽग्निरध्वरेष्वीड्यः सर्वैर्यज्वभिः स्तुत्यो होता देवानामाह्वाता विशि जने मंद्रतमः स्तुत्यतमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Viśvasāman: this is an address to the seer. Like Atri, sing forth (prārca, pragāya) to Agni, whose radiance purifies (pāvakaśociṣe). That Agni who is to be praised (īḍya) at the sacrifices by all sacrificers, the hotṛ, the summoner of the gods, is the most praiseworthy among the people (viś).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वसामन् (*viśvasāman*)—O seer named Viśvasāman
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- अध्वरेषु (*adhvareṣu*)—at the sacrifices
- ईड्यः (*īḍyaḥ*)—is worthy of worship
- होता (*hotā*)—is of the nature of the hotṛ
- विशि (*viśi*)—among all people
- मंद्रतमः (*maṃdratamaḥ*)—is the most praiseworthy; such
- पावकशोचिषे (*pāvakaśociṣe*)—addressing Agni, whose radiance purifies
- अत्रिवत् (*atrivat*)—as the seer Atri recited
- प्र अर्च (*pra arca*)—recite an excellent praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O seer named Viśvasāman, as the seer Atri recited, recite an excellent praise addressed to Agni, who is most worthy of worship at the sacrifices, is of the nature of the hotṛ, is the most praiseworthy among all people, and has a purifying radiance.

##### English translation
Sing, Vishwasaman, like Atri, (a hymn) to the dispencer of purifying light : (to him) who is to be praised at sacrifices, the invoker of the gods, the most adorable by man.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न्य१॒॑ग्निं जा॒तवे॑दसं॒ धधा॑ता दे॒वमृ॒त्विजं॑ ।  
> प्र य॒ज्ञ ए॑त्वानु॒षग॒द्या दे॒वव्य॑चस्तमः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 112

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नि । अग्निं । जातवेदसं । दधात । देवं । ऋत्विजं ।  
> प्र । यज्ञः । एतु । आनुषक् । अद्य । देवव्यचतमः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे यजमाना जातवेदसं जातप्रज्ञं जातधनं वा देवं द्योतमानमृत्विजमृतयष्टारमग्निं नि दधात । निधत्त । किंचदेवव्यचस्तमो देवानामाप्ततमो
> यज्ञो यज्ञसाधनमस्माभिर्दीयमानं हविरानुषक् अनुषक्तं यथा भवति
> तथाद्यास्मिन्नहनि तमग्निं प्रैतु । गच्छतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O sacrificers, establish (ni dadhāta) Agni Jātavedas, the one endowed with knowledge or possessor of wealth that has arisen, the shining one, the priest (ṛtvij), the sacrificer at the proper time. Moreover, may the sacrifice, that is, the oblation given by us as the means of sacrifice, which is the dearest (devavyacastama) to the gods, go forth to that Agni today, on this day, continuously (ānuṣak).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—(O sacrificers) endowed with the power of knowing everything that has arisen
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—the shining
- ऋत्विजं (*ṛtvijaṃ*)—in the form of the chief priest of the sacrifice
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- नि दधात (*ni dadhāta*)—establish (on the sacrificial ground)
- देवव्यचस्तमः (*devavyacastamaḥ*)—most dear to all the gods
- यज्ञः (*yajñaḥ*)—the oblation offered in the sacrifice
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—continuously
- अद्य (*adya*)—in the sacrifice
- प्र एतु (*pra etu*)—may it join (Agni)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sacrificers, establish on the sacrificial ground Agni, who has the power of knowing everything that has arisen, who is shining and is in the form of the chief priest of the sacrifice. May the oblation offered in the sacrifice, most dear to all the gods, continuously join Agni in this sacrifice.

##### English translation
Cherish the divine Agni, by whom all that exists is known, the priest (of the rite): may the sacrifice most suitable for the gods duly this day proceed to them.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> चि॒कि॒त्विन्म॑नसं त्वा दे॒वं मर्ता॑स ऊ॒तये॑ ।  
> वरे॑ण्यस्य॒ तेव॑स इया॒नासो॑ अमन्महि ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 113

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> चिकित्त्वमनसं । त्वा । देवं । मर्तासः । ऊतये ।  
> वरेण्यस्य । ते । अवसः । इयानासः । अमन्महि ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने चिकित्विन्मनसं । चिकित्विज्ज्ञानन्मनो यस्यासौ चिकित्विन्मनाः । तं देवं
> त्वा त्वामियानास उपगच्छंतो मर्तासो मनुष्या वयमूतये रक्षणार्थं
> वरेण्यस्य संभजनीयस्य ते तवावसोऽवसे तर्पणायामन्महि । स्तुमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you who are cikitvin-manas, one whose mind is knowing. We mortals, men, approaching you, the god, for protection, praise you for the satisfaction of your favour (avas), you who are to be shared in (varenya), worthy of being resorted to.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- चिकित्सत्मनसं (*cikitsatmanasaṃ*)—possessed of mental power that knows everything
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—divine in nature
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—for protection
- इयानासः (*iyānāsaḥ*)—approaching
- मर्तासः (*martāsaḥ*)—we men
- वरेण्यस्य (*vareṇyasya*)—worthy of worship and honoured
- ते (*te*)—your
- अवसः (*avasaḥ*)—for satisfaction
- अमन्महि (*amanmahi*)—we praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, we men, who approach you for protection, you who are possessed of a mental power that knows everything and are divine in nature, praise you for the satisfaction of you who are worthy of worship and honoured.

##### English translation
Men have recourse to you divine Agni, who are of intelligent mind, for security : we praise you who are most excellent, seeking your protection.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॑ चिकि॒द्द्य१॒ं॑स्य न॑ इ॒दं वचः॑ सहस्य ।  
> तं त्वा॑ सुशिप्र दंपते॒ स्तोमै॑र्वर्धं॒त्यत्र॑यो  
> गी॒र्भिः शुं॑भं॒त्यत्र॑यः ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 114

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । चिकिद्दि । अस्य । नः । इदं । वचः । सहस्य ।  
> तं । त्वा । सुशिप्र । दंपते । स्तोमैः । वर्धंति । अत्रयः । गीभिः ।  
> शुंभंति । अत्रयः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने हे सहस्य सहसो बलस्य पुत्र नोऽस्मदीयमस्येदं परिचरणमिदं
> वचः स्तोत्रं चिकिद्दि । जानीहि । हे सुशिप्र । शोभने शिप्रे हनू नासिके वा
> यस्यासौ सुशिप्रः । तस्य संबोधनं । सुशिप्र सुहनो दंपते गृहपते तं त्वामत्रयोऽत्रिपुत्राः स्तोमैः स्तोत्रैर्वर्धंति । वर्धयंति । अत्रयो गीर्भिः
> शुंभंति । अलंकुर्वंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, O Sahasya, son of strength (sahas), know this our service, this speech, this praise. O Suśipra, the one whose jaws or nostrils (śipra) are handsome; the word is an address to him. O Suśipra, of fine jaws, O Dampati, lord of the house, the sons of Atri make you grow with their stomas, praises; the sons of Atri adorn you with their words.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सहस्य (*sahasya*)—O you, the son of strength
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- अस्य (*asya*)—belonging to this sacrifice
- इदं वचः (*idaṃ vacaḥ*)—this praise, which is worthy of worship
- चिकिद्दि (*cikiddi*)—know
- सुशिप्र (*suśipra*)—you who have excellent cheeks
- दंपते (*daṃpate*)—and who are the lord of the house
- तं (*taṃ*)—you
- अत्रयः (*atrayaḥ*)—the sons of Atri
- स्तोमैः (*stomaiḥ*)—with hymns
- वर्धंति (*vardhaṃti*)—make you grow strong and increase
- अत्रयः (*atrayaḥ*)—the same sons of Atri
- गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)—with words of praise
- शुंभंति (*śuṃbhaṃti*)—adorn

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, son of strength, know the nature of this praise of ours, which belongs to this sacrifice and is worthy of worship. The sons of Atri make you, who have excellent cheeks and are the lord of the house, grow strong with hymns. The same sons of Atri adorn you with words of praise. (They praise you.)

##### English translation
Agni, son of strength recognize the words of this our (laudation): handsome-chinned, lord of the dwelling, the sons of Atri exalt you, such (as you are), by their praises : they embellish you by their hymns.

### Printed p. 115
## Sūkta 23 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-third sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्ने सहंतमिति चतुर्ऋचं नवमं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । अग्ने
> द्युन्नो विश्वचर्षणिरिति । द्युन्न ऋषिः । चतुर्थी पंक्तिः । आद्यास्ति स्रोऽनुष्टुभः ।
> अग्निर्देवता ॥ प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोर्दशसूक्तांतःपातित्वादुक्तो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning "Agne sahantam" has four Ṛks and is the ninth sūkta. Here the Anukramaṇikā says "Agne dyunno viśvacarṣaṇiḥ". **NOTE** The seer is Dyunna (Dyumna); the fourth verse is paṅkti and the first three are anuṣṭubh. The deity is Agni. Since the sūkta is included among the ten sūktas, its use in the Prātaranuvāka and the Āśvina-śastra has been stated.

Translation (anuvāda): This sūkta beginning "Agne sahantam" is the ninth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has four Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said "Agne dyumno viśvacarṣaṇiḥ". The seer of this sūkta is Dyumna Viśvacarṣaṇi; Agni is the deity. The first three Ṛks of this sūkta are in the anuṣṭubh metre; the fourth is in the paṅkti metre. It has been stated earlier that this sūkta is used for the recitation of the Prātaranuvāka and the mantras of the Āśvina-śastra.

Sūkta 23
Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 23
Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 1; Varga 15
Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4
Ṛṣi: Dyumna Viśvacarṣaṇi
Devatā: Agni
Chandas: 1–3 Anuṣṭubh; 4 Paṅkti

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ सहं॑त॒मा भ॑र द्यु॒म्नस्य॑ प्रा॒सहा॑ र॒यिं ।  
> विश्वा॒ यश्च॑र्ष॒णीर॒भ्या॒३॒॑३सा वाजे॑षु सा॒सह॑त् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । सहंतं । आ । भर । द्युम्नस्य । प्रसहा । रयिं ।  
> विश्वाः । यः । चर्षणीः । अभि । आसा । वाजेषु । ससहत् ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 116

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने प्रासहा प्रकृष्टेन बलेन सहंतं शत्रुनभिभवंतं रयिं पुत्रं
> द्युम्नस्य द्युम्नाय मम ऋषय आ भर । आहर ॥ चतुर्थ्यर्थे षष्ठी ॥ एतदेव
> विवृणोति। यः पुत्र आसास्येन स्तोत्रेण युक्तः सन् वाजेषु युद्धेष्वभि अभिमुख्येन गतान् विश्वाः सर्वान् चर्षणीर्मनुष्यान् शत्रुन् सासहत् अभिभवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, bring (ā bhara) to me, Dyumna, a son (rayi) who overcomes his foes with surpassing strength, as wealth. Here the genitive stands in the sense of the dative. He now explains this: the son who, endowed with praise (āsā, by the mouth), subdues in battles all the men, the enemies, who come against him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which son
- आसा (*āsā*)—having a mouth (filled with your praise)
- वाजेषु (*vājeṣu*)—in battles
- अभि (*abhi*)—who has come to meet him
- विश्वाः (*viśvāḥ*)—all
- चर्षणीः (*carṣaṇīḥ*)—men (the enemies)
- सासहत् (*sāsahat*)—overcomes, such a one
- प्रासहा (*prāsahā*)—with superior strength
- सहंतं (*sahaṃtaṃ*)—who confronts enemies
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—a son
- द्युम्नस्य (*dyumnasya*)—to me, named Dyumna
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, provide to me, named Dyumna, such a son of excellent strength as, with a mouth that praises you alone in battles, has the power to slay all the enemies who face him.

##### English translation
Bestow, Agni, upon Dyumna a son, overcoming foes by his prowess ; one who may with glory subdue all men in battle.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तम॑ग्ने पृतना॒षहं॑ र॒यिं स॑हस्व॒ आ भ॑र ।  
> त्वं हि स॒त्यो अद्भु॑तो दा॒ता वाज॑स्य॒ गोम॑तः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । अग्ने । पृतनासहं । रयिं । सहस्वः । आ । भर ।  
> त्वं । हि । सत्यः । अद्भुतः । दाता । वाजस्य । गोऽमतः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 117

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सहस्वो बलवन् पृतनासहं पृतनाः सेना अभिभवितारं रयिं
> पुत्रं त्वमा भर । आहर । त्वं हि सत्यः सत्यभूतोऽद्भुतो महान् गोमतो
> गोभिर्युक्तस्य वाजस्यान्नस्य दाता ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, O mighty one, bring (ā bhara) a son who overcomes armies (pṛtanā), the enemy hosts. For you are true, in truth wonderful, great, the giver of food (vāja) accompanied by cattle.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सहस्वः (*sahasvaḥ*)—endowed with strength
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- पृतनासहं (*pṛtanāsahaṃ*)—having the strength to overcome the enemy army
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—a son
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—obtain for me
- त्वं हि (*tvaṃ hi*)—you indeed
- सत्यः (*satyaḥ*)—are true in nature
- अद्भुतः (*adbhutaḥ*)—are of great form
- गोमतः (*gomataḥ*)—accompanied by cattle
- वाजस्य (*vājasya*)—of food
- दाता (*dātā*)—and the giver

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, endowed with strength, provide me with a son who has the strength to overcome the enemy army. You are true in nature, great in form, and the giver of food accompanied by cattle.

##### English translation
Mighty Agni, grant us a son able to encounter hosts ; for you are true and wonderful, and the giver of food with cattle.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विश्वे॒ हि त्वा॑ स॒जोष॑सो॒ जना॑सो वृ॒क्तब॑र्हिषः ।  
> होता॑रं॒ सद्म॑सु प्रि॒यं व्यंति॒ वार्या॑ पु॒रु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विश्वे । हि । त्वा । सजोषसः । जनासः । वृक्तबर्हिषः ।  
> होतारं । सद्मसु । प्रियं । व्यंति । वार्या । पुरु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सजोषसः सह प्रीयमाणा व्यक्तबर्हिषो लूनबर्हिषो विश्वे
> सर्वॆ जनासो जना ऋत्विजः सद्मसु यज्ञगृहेषु होतारं देवानामाह्वा-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, all the people, the priests (ṛtvij), being pleased together (sajoṣasaḥ), with the sacred grass (barhis) cut, at the sacrificial houses (sadman), you, the hotṛ, the summoner of the gods…

### Printed p. 118

> तारं प्रियं सर्वॆषां प्रीतिकरं त्वा त्वां पुरु पुरूणि बहूनि वार्या वरणीयानि धनानि व्यंति । याचंते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

…you, who are dear (priya), pleasing to all, they ask for (vyanti) much (puru) choice wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सजोषसः (*sajoṣasaḥ*)—having equal devotion and affection
- व्यक्तबर्हिषः (*vyaktabarhiṣaḥ*)—who have prepared the cut darbha grass as seats
- विश्वे जनासः (*viśve janāsaḥ*)—all the priests
- सद्मसु (*sadmasu*)—in the sacrificial houses
- होतारं (*hotāraṃ*)—of the nature of the hotṛ
- प्रियं (*priyaṃ*)—and dear to all
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- पुरु (*puru*)—abundant
- वार्या (*vāryā*)—and desired wealth
- व्यंति (*vyaṃti*)—they ask for

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, all the priests, who have equal devotion and affection, and who have cut the darbha grass and arranged excellent seats, ask you, who are of the nature of the hotṛ and dear to all, in the sacrificial houses for abundant and desired wealth.

##### English translation
All men concurring in satisfaction, bearing the clipt sacred grass, solicit you, as the kind invoker of the gods, to the chambers (of sacrifice) for infinite wealth.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स हि ष्मा॑ वि॒श्वच॑र्षणिर॒भिमा॑ति॒ सहो॑ द॒धे ।  
> अग्न॑ ए॒षु क्षये॒ष्वा रे॒वन्नः॑ शुक्र दीदिहि द्यु॒मत्पा॑वक  
> दीदिहि ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । हि । स्म । विश्वचर्षणिः । अभिमाति । सहः । दधे ।  
> अग्ने । एषु । क्षयेषु । आ । रेवत् । नः । शुक्र । दीदिहि । द्युमत् ।  
> पावक । दीदिहि ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 119

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने स लोकेषु प्रसिद्धो विश्वचर्षणिर्यषिरभिमाति शत्रूणां हिंसकं
> सहो बलं दधे । धारयतु । हि स्मृति पूरणौ । हे शुक्र शोचिष्मन् त्वं नोऽस्मदीयेषु क्षयेषु गृहेषु रेवत् धनयुक्तं यथा भवति तथा दीदिहि । दीप्यस्व
> हॆ पावक पापानां शोधकाग्ने त्वं द्युमद्दीप्तियुक्तं यशोयुक्तं च धीदिहि ।
> दीप्यस्व ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, may that well-known sage Viśvacarṣaṇi hold (dadhe) strength (sahas) that destroys enemies. **NOTE** O bright (śukra) Agni, possessed of splendour, shine in our houses (kṣaya) in such a way that they are rich in wealth (revat). O Pāvaka, Agni, purifier of sins, shine with brilliance and with glory (dyumat).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सः (*saḥ*)—the well-known
- विश्वचर्षणिः (*viśvacarṣaṇiḥ*)—the seer named Viśvacarṣaṇi
- अभिमाति (*abhimāti*)—destroying enemies
- सहः (*sahaḥ*)—strength
- दधे (*dadhe*)—may he obtain
- शुक्र (*śukra*)—you, endowed with radiance
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- एषु क्षयेषु (*eṣu kṣayeṣu*)—in these sacrificial houses
- रेवत् (*revat*)—so that there is abundance of wealth
- आ दीदिहि (*ā dīdihi*)—shine
- पावक (*pāvaka*)—O Agni, purifier
- द्युमत् (*dyumat*)—as the most brilliant
- दीदिहि (*dīdihi*)—blaze forth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, may the seer well known by the name Viśvacarṣaṇi obtain strength that destroys enemies. You, endowed with radiance, shine in these our sacrificial houses so that wealth may abound. O Agni, the purifier, spread your rays everywhere and blaze so as to be most brilliant.

##### English translation
May the (sage) on whom all men rely possess foe-subduing strength : radiant Agni, so shine in our habitations that they may abound in riches : shine, purifying Agni, dispensing light.

## Sūkta 24 (Maṇḍala 5)

The twenty-fourth sūkta.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्ने त्वं न इति चतुर्ऋचं दशमं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका । अग्ने त्वं
> गौपायना लौपायना वा बंधुः सुबंधुः श्रुतबंधुर्विप्रबंधुश्चकर्चा द्वैपदमिति । विंशतिका द्विपदा विराज इत्युक्त्वाच्च । अग्निर्देवता ॥ महापितृयज्ञ
> आहवनीयं प्रति गच्छंत ऋत्विज इदं सूक्तं जपेयुः । तथा च सूत्रितं । अधैनमभिसमायंति मा प्र गामाग्ने त्वं न इति जपंतः । आ. २-१९ । इति ॥ दशरात्रे

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning "Agne tvaṃ naḥ" has four Ṛks and is the tenth sūkta. Here the Anukramaṇikā says: "Agne tvaṃ, Gaupāyanas or Laupāyanas: Bandhu, Subandhu, Śrutabandhu, Viprabandhu; he sang a dvipada", and it states that the twentieth (verse) is a dvipadā virāj. The deity is Agni. At the Mahāpitṛyajña the priests, going towards the Āhavanīya fire, are to mutter this sūkta; so it is laid down in the sūtra: "Then they approach him, muttering मा प्र गामाग्ने त्वं न इति जपंतः (*mā pra gāmāgne tvaṃ na iti japaṃtaḥ*)", Ā. 2-19.

### Printed p. 120

> षष्ठेऽहनि तृतीयसवने मैत्रावरुणस्याग्ने त्वमिति द्वे तं त्वा शोचिष्ठेत्येका ।
> ऐवं मिलितो द्वैपदस्तृचः स्तोत्रियः । तथैव सूत्रितं । अग्ने त्वं नो अंतमोऽग्ने भव सुषमिधा । आ ८-२ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

On the sixth day of the Daśarātra, at the third pressing, the Maitrāvaruṇa priest recites the two Ṛks beginning "Agne tvam" together with the one Ṛk "Taṃ tvā śociṣṭha"; thus combined, the three form a tṛca of the dvipada kind, and it is the stotriya. So it is laid down in the sūtra: अग्ने त्वं नो अंतमोऽग्ने भव सुषमिधा (*agne tvaṃ no aṃtamo 'gne bhava suṣamidhā*), Ā. 8-2.

This sūkta, beginning "Agne tvaṃ naḥ", is the tenth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has four Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: "Agne tvam, Gaupāyanas or Laupāyanas, Bandhu, Subandhu, Śrutabandhu, Viprabandhu; a single Ṛk each; dvipada". "Viṃśatikā dvipadā virājaḥ" means that a Ṛk made of two half-Ṛks of twenty syllables is called a dvipadā virāj; this is the mark of this metre. The seers of the four Ṛks of this sūkta are, in order, the four, Bandhu, Subandhu, Śrutabandhu and Viprabandhu, or else the two, Gaupāyana and Laupāyana; the deity is Agni. That the priests are to mutter this sūkta when going towards the Āhavanīya fire at the Mahāpitṛyajña is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra: अथैनमभिसमायंति मा प्र गामाग्ने त्वं न इति जपंतः (*athainamabhisamāyaṃti mā pra gāmāgne tvaṃ na iti japaṃtaḥ*) (Ā. 2-19).
